## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route: an agenda that is repeated as a tracker (slides 2, 3, 10, 39, 48), consistent section tags, and three pillars each opened by a photo divider and followed by goal tables with 2019 baselines and 2025 targets. Visual identity is coherent and polished, and slides 26 and 23 show strong chart craft with readable headline numbers. The weaknesses are density and legibility: many pages (7, 15, 16, 18, 41, 45, 47) carry small body text and footnotes that cannot be read in a room, and several are walls of bullets or maps with little hierarchy. Slide 8 is blank, slide 2 shows 'GENDA' with the A cropped, and slides 3 and 10 repeat the agenda tracker without adding content.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the agenda tracker with presenter photos, the goal-and-indicator table header used on slides 14, 22, 29, 32 and 35, the waterfall on slide 26 with its single takeaway box, and the lever 1/2/3 structure on slides 23 to 25.

**Most useful repair:** Fix the blank slide 8 and the clipped 'GENDA' title, and enlarge or cut the small text on slides 7, 15, 16, 18, 41, 45 and 47.

**What would change the score:** I would split or reduce the densest case-study and governance slides to one message each and give the action titles on pages like 13, 42 and 43 a stated conclusion instead of a topic label.

_Confidence medium. 55 of 55 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and an agenda naming the presenting executives, then introduces Verallia as a global glass packaging maker, covering its end-market split, footprint, values and purpose. It moves through a materiality exercise and the company's membership in the Ellen MacArthur Foundation. The main body covers the three CSR pillars in order: enhancing the circularity of glass packaging (cullet collection, recycling capacity and furnace use, plus reuse), significantly reducing CO2 emissions (the 2030 target, the levers to reach it, green electricity, lightweighting and tree planting), and providing a safe and inclusive workplace (accident frequency, diversity, employee shareholding and community support). Governance follows, covering board composition, committees, ESG oversight, executive pay, compliance, whistleblowing and responsible purchasing. The deck closes with a conclusion summarizing the ESG ambition and ratings, then a Q&A slide and appendices with the goal tables and historical data. Slides that show the approach well include the CO2 reduction action plan waterfall, the green electricity target chart and the safety roadmap maturity ladder.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does Verallia's ESG Strategy Presentation from January 21, 2021 have?**

The deck has 55 pages in total, as shown in its slide count. The title slide is slide 1 and the agenda appears on slide 3.

**What CO2 emissions reduction target does Verallia set for 2030 in its ESG strategy presentation?**

Verallia's goal is to reduce absolute CO2 emissions by 27.5% by 2030 vs 2019, in line with Science-Based Targets (slide 22). The slide shows scopes 1 and 2 emissions falling from 3,090 k in 2019 to 2,240 k in 2030 (slide 22).

**What does Verallia's ESG strategy presentation say about external cullet usage targets?**

The rate of external cullet usage in Verallia's glass production sites was 49% in 2019, with a 2025 target of 59% (slide 14). The goal is to increase the rate of use of external cullet by 10 pts vs 2019 (slide 14).

**How is Verallia's ESG strategy presentation structured?**

The agenda on slide 3 lists five sections: Introduction, CSR Strategy: 3 Main Pillars, Governance, and Conclusion. The CSR strategy section covers three pillars: enhancing the circularity of glass packaging, significantly reducing CO2 emissions across operations, and providing a safe and inclusive place of work (slide 11).

**Layout**

Centred title composition over a full-bleed photograph within a thin white margin (about 2.6% left and right, 4.6% top and bottom). The title block is centred horizontally at about x 50 and sits across the vertical middle (y 46 to 77). The date sits centred below it at y 74, and the verallia logo is centred near the bottom at y 88. There is no footer text and no slide number.

**Visuals**

A photograph of a glass bottling line: a row of dark green and olive wine bottles standing upright on a stainless steel conveyor ring, seen from a low angle. The bottles recede to the right and their necks are cut off at the top of the frame. A blurred yellow and white background sits at upper right. Lighting is soft, natural and even, with specular highlights on the glass. The dominant colours are dark olive green, grey steel and muted teal. The photo is shown inside a white margin rather than edge to edge. Three large teal (#01A5A5) translucent organic curved shapes overlay it: one covering the upper left, one covering the lower right, and one at upper left over the bottles. A translucent teal rectangle sits behind the presenter name. A white horizontal rule runs on each side of the name. The verallia logo appears at bottom centre: a stylised green and teal leaf-like mark with the lowercase white wordmark 'verallia'.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF margin with a full-bleed bottling-line photograph behind the content, overlaid with teal #01A5A5 translucent shapes. Photographic cover slide with teal translucent organic shapes overlaid on the image. Background is white, with the photograph covering the slide area inside its margin. Title is white (#FFFFFF) uppercase Bahnschrift regular, with a subtle drop shadow, at about 9.3% of slide height. The presenter name is white uppercase Bahnschrift regular at about 5.2% of slide height. The role line is white Bahnschrift regular in mixed case at about 3.0%. The date is white Bahnschrift regular at about 2.6%. Accent colour is teal #01A5A5 at various opacities. Shapes are smooth, freeform, curved and unrounded; no shadows on shapes and no gradients.

**Text**

GENDA / INTRODUCTION / CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS / GOVERNANCE / CONCLUSION / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Laetitia FABRE / Head of CSR / Romain BARRAL / Director of Operations / Denis MICHEL / Secretary General / Wendy KOOL-FOULON / General Counsel / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 2

**Layout**

Four equal columns, each with a rounded blob image above a short teal rule, a grey section label and portrait or name blocks below, centred on the column axis. The title GENDA sits top-left at about x 9.9%, y 5.8%, left-aligned; a white letter A (x 6.3%) precedes it and is invisible on the white ground. The footer text sits bottom-left at x 2.8%, y 96.7%, the Verallia logo is centred at the bottom around x 46%, and the slide number 2 sits bottom-right on a teal corner shape. Margins are roughly 3% left and right.

**Visuals**

Four blob-framed pictures, one per column. Column 1 (INTRODUCTION): a photograph of green, olive and amber wine and beer bottles and glass jars standing in a row on white, lit evenly from the front, cropped to an organic oval-triangle window. Column 2 (CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS): a split photograph, left half an orange-lit glass bottle with a white thread loop, right half a tree trunk with green ivy leaves, divided by a vertical seam. Column 3 (GOVERNANCE): a flat illustration of a ring of stylised hands in yellow, teal, olive, orange and dark blue-grey, meeting at the centre in a circular arrangement on white. Column 4 (CONCLUSION): a photograph of many empty glass bottles in green, cobalt blue and clear glass on a light grey background with a wooden surface, lit from the side. Below, six circular black-and-white portrait photos on teal blob backing: Michel GIANNUZZI (man, smiling, dark suit, white shirt, grey background, head and shoulders, appears twice), Laetitia FABRE (woman, dark hair, smiling, grey top, head and shoulders), Romain BARRAL (man, grey hair, dark jacket, head and shoulders), Denis MICHEL (man, short grey hair, dark suit, white shirt, head and shoulders), Wendy KOOL-FOULON (woman, long blonde hair, smiling, head and shoulders). Each portrait is cropped to a rounded blob with a teal shape offset behind it. Bottom centre: the Verallia logo, a green-teal overlapping-leaf mark with the grey wordmark 'verallia'. A thin teal bar runs along the bottom-left edge under the footer. Corner shape bottom-right, teal at 25% opacity.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title teal #01A5A5, large, regular-weight uppercase sans-serif (Bahnschrift), about 10% of slide height. Section labels mid-grey #7F7F7F, uppercase, regular weight, about 3% of slide height. Names teal #01A5A5, regular weight, about 1.7% of height; roles teal #01A5A5, about 1.5%. Footer light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.5%. Slide number white, about 2.2%. Accent colour teal #01A5A5 with lighter teal #78CFCF blobs and grey #E9EAED / #E4E6E9 blob backing. Blob shapes are soft organic rounded triangles with no outlines and no shadows. Teal rules are short solid bars about 0.4% of height thick. No gradients.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear route: an agenda that is repeated as a tracker (slides 2, 3, 10, 39, 48), consistent section tags, and three pillars each opened by a photo divider and followed by goal tables with 2019 baselines and 2025 targets.
- Review: Slide 8 is blank, slide 2 shows 'GENDA' with the A cropped, and slides 3 and 10 repeat the agenda tracker without adding content.

**Text**

AGENDA / INTRODUCTION / CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS / GOVERNANCE / CONCLUSION / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Laetitia FABRE / Head of CSR / Romain BARRAL / Director of Operations / Denis MICHEL / Secretary General / Wendy KOOL-FOULON / General Counsel / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / verallia / 3

**Layout**

Single white slide with the title AGENDA in the upper left at about 10% from the left edge and 6% from the top. Four equal columns of section cards sit across the middle, each with a blob-shaped picture frame, a short horizontal rule, an uppercase section label, and presenter portraits with name and role beneath; the first column is the active one, and the other three are faded to grey. The footer runs along the bottom: a left-aligned confidentiality line, a centred Verallia logo, and a page number at the lower right on a teal corner shape.

**Visuals**

Four blob-shaped picture frames, one per agenda section, at the same height across the slide. Column 1 (active, INTRODUCTION): a teal #01A5A5 organic ring around a photograph of green, amber and brown glass bottles and jars standing on a white background, seen at a close eye-level frame with the bottles filling the frame and slightly cropped on the left and right edges; the photo is full colour. Column 2 (CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS): a grey ring around a greyscale split photograph, left half a clear glass bottle with a ribbed surface, right half a close-up of leaves and a textured surface, with a white curved line tracing an infinity-like loop across the middle. Column 3 (GOVERNANCE): a grey ring around a white-on-white line icon of a circular gear-like wheel with four spokes ending in cogs, drawn in light grey. Column 4 (CONCLUSION): a grey ring around a greyscale photograph of many wine and glass bottles with a jar in the foreground, faded to light grey. Portraits, each in a blob-shaped crop with a grey or teal backdrop: Michel GIANNUZZI (CEO, active, colour-tinted crop, smiling bald man in dark suit and open-necked shirt, teal blob behind), Laetitia FABRE (faded greyscale, woman with dark hair), Romain BARRAL (faded greyscale, smiling man with short grey hair in dark jacket), Denis MICHEL (faded greyscale, smiling man with grey hair and dark jacket), Wendy KOOL-FOULON (faded greyscale, woman with long light hair), Michel GIANNUZZI (CEO, faded greyscale, same portrait as the active one). Verallia logo: a green overlapping-circles mark followed by the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey. Lighting is soft and even throughout; no overlays except the white 70% veil over the faded columns.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light teal #01A5A5 corner shape at 25% opacity in the lower right. White background. Title in a geometric sans-serif (Bahnschrift-like, DIN-style), regular weight, all capitals, in teal #01A5A5, about 10% of slide height. Active section label INTRODUCTION in medium-grey #7F7F7F, the inactive labels in #7F7F7F under a faded white veil, uppercase, about 3% of height. Portrait names in #7F7F7F with the active name in teal #01A5A5; roles about 1.5% of height in the same colours. Footer text in light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.5% of height. Page number white on teal, about 2.2% of height. Accent elements: a short teal #01A5A5 rule above the active label and grey #BFBFBF rules above the inactive labels, all about 1% of height thick. Blob frames have soft organic shapes with no hard corners; no shadows and no gradients; the corner shape bottom right is a light teal #01A5A5 at 25% opacity. A teal #78CFCE bar sits at the bottom left under the footer text.

### Slide 4

**Text**

1 / INTRODUCTION / A GLOBAL LEADER IN GLASS PACKAGING / Diversified and balanced end-markets / 2019 Glass packaging(1) sales split by end-market / Food / 17% / Soft drinks / 12% / Beer / 13% / Spirits / 13% / Sparkling wine / 12% / Still wine / 33% / 11 / countries / 10,000 / employees / 16 / billion / bottles/jars p.a. / 32 / glass plants / 8 / cullet recycling / centers / 3 / decoration plants / 57 / furnaces / N° 1 / in Europe(2) / 90% of 2019 sales / N° 2 / in Latin America(3) / 10% of 2019 sales / N° 3 / Globally / Sources: Companies public information, management estimates and Advancy (IPO related study) / Notes: (1) For bottles and jars only (97% of total Verallia sales) / (2) Based on 2019 sales; “Europe” using each company’s definition and management estimates / (3) Based on 2019 volumes in Argentina, Brazil and Chile / verallia / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 4

**Layout**

Two-column-plus-hero layout on a white slide. The title sits top-left at about 4.4% from the top with a 2.5% left margin, under a teal section tag ‘1 INTRODUCTION’ in the top-left corner. Left column holds a donut chart with its heading (about x 5.8–34%); a centred white rounded blob with seven KPI bubbles and a central Verallia logo sits in the middle (x 34–78%, y 17.8–89.6%); the right column stacks three large N° figures (x 83–97%). Sources line sits bottom-left at y 85.9%, notes bottom-right at x 66–98%, footer bottom-left at y 96.7%, logo centred bottom at y 94.6%, page number bottom-right on a pale teal corner shape. Margins are about 3% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Donut chart. Title: none printed as chart title; chart heading is ‘Diversified and balanced end-markets’ with subtitle ‘2019 Glass packaging(1) sales split by end-market’. Type: donut with centre hole of about 50% diameter; no axes, no units. Segments clockwise from top: Still wine 33% (dark green #009362, label inside segment in white), Sparkling wine 12% (green #58AB27), Spirits 13% (teal #00A7A7), Beer 13% (yellow #F9B200), Soft drinks 12% (orange #EA640D), Food 17% (magenta #AE0079). Each segment has its category name in its own colour placed outside the ring, and its percentage in white inside the ring. No legend; no highlighted points. Central blob graphic: seven teal rounded-blob bubbles, each with a white bold number and a grey label beneath, connected by thin light-teal straight lines (#00B3B0, about 1 px) to a central Verallia glass-droplet logo: 11 countries (upper left), 10,000 employees (top), 16 billion bottles/jars p.a. (upper right), 8 cullet recycling centers (right), 57 furnaces (bottom), 3 decoration plants (lower left), 32 glass plants (left). Bubbles are two overlapping teal blobs (#00B3B0 at 27% and 63% opacity). Central logo: three overlapping translucent glass drops in teal and green with white ‘verallia’ lowercase wordmark. Footer logo: small Verallia drop mark plus wordmark ‘verallia’ in dark teal-grey. Decorative corner shape bottom-right in #01A5A5 at 25% opacity. Icon-like small ornaments: none.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF, full bleed.

### Slide 5

**Text**

1 / INTRODUCTION / OUR VALUES / CARE / for customer / RESPECT / people, laws and / the environment / EMPOWERMENT / and accountability / TEAMWORK / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 5

**Layout**

Two-by-two symmetrical grid centred on the Verallia logo, with four teal blobs on the inner corners and a text label beside each blob (CARE and EMPOWERMENT left, RESPECT and TEAMWORK right), split by thin light-teal crosshair lines. The section tag INTRODUCTION sits in a teal box at top left with the number 1 beside it, and the title OUR VALUES is left-aligned at top left below it. The footer has the source line at bottom left, a small Verallia logo at bottom centre, a page number 5 in the bottom-right corner, and a teal bar at bottom left. Margins are about 3 percent on the left.

**Visuals**

Four solid teal blob shapes, each an irregular rounded pebble-like form in #00B3B0, each carrying a white pictogram. Top-left blob: white wine bottle and jar with teal heart symbols on their labels. Top-right blob: white pictogram of a person, two bottles and glasses, and a leaf, above an open white hand. Bottom-left blob: white pictogram of a person beside a white shelf or cabinet with a grid of small bottles. Bottom-right blob: two white person silhouettes with arms spread, standing either side of a white bottle. Centre: full-colour Verallia logo (three overlapping translucent green-teal glass drops with dark grey wordmark verallia) placed over the crosshair intersection, overlapping the inner edges of the top blobs. Four thin light-teal lines (#9ED8D8, about 0.1 percent width) form the crosshair: two horizontal lines from the logo outward and two vertical lines from the top and bottom of the centre. Bottom-centre footer logo is a small version of the same logo. Nothing is cropped; all are boxed on the white background, not full-bleed. No photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). Titles and labels in teal #01A5A5 (labels CARE, RESPECT, EMPOWERMENT, TEAMWORK) and the title OUR VALUES in teal #01A5A5. Body labels in mid grey #7F7F7F. Section tag box fill #78CFCF with white text INTRODUCTION. Typeface is Bahnschrift, a DIN-style condensed-leaning sans-serif in regular and medium weights; title in caps at about 5.9 percent of slide height, labels about 3.7 percent. Blob shapes have no outline, no shadow, no gradient. Bottom-right corner shape is a light teal quarter-circle fill #01A5A5 at 25 percent opacity. Footer bar is solid #01A5A5.

### Slide 6

**Text**

1 / INTRODUCTION / OUR PURPOSE / Re-imagine glass / for a sustainable future / 6 / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Single full-width composition on a white slide. A teal section tab and the all-caps title sit in the top-left margin, with the title starting about 2.5% from the left. A full-bleed photographic band runs across the middle, with a dark translucent strip laid over it carrying the quotation. The footer has a small grey confidentiality-style line at bottom-left, the Verallia logo centred at the bottom, a teal bar under the footer text, and the page number in a pale teal corner shape at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Photograph, full-bleed across the slide width in a horizontal band from about 20% to 84% of slide height. Subject: left half shows a heap of crushed green, white and brown glass cullet, sharp and in focus; right half shows several olive-green glass bottles standing upright, their rims and necks blurred. Composition: landscape band, bottles cropped at the right edge, cullet filling the left. Lighting: soft, even, diffuse daylight. Dominant colours: sage green, off-white, dark olive. A black overlay at 44% opacity covers a horizontal strip from 35% to 75% of slide height across the full width, darkening the photo behind the quotation. Two large white opening quotation mark graphics sit at the left of the strip, about 12% to 26% of slide width, overlapping the cullet. Verallia logo: teal and green overlapping rounded blobs with the lowercase word 'verallia' in dark grey, centred near the bottom. No other icons.

**Design**

Background is white #FFFFFF. Titles: teal #01A5A5, Bahnschrift regular, all caps, title cap height about 5.9% of slide height. Section tab: light teal #78CFCF fill with white all-caps 'INTRODUCTION' in Bahnschrift regular, about 2% of slide height; the section number '1' is teal #78CFCF, Bahnschrift. Quote text: white #FFFFFF Bahnschrift, 'Re-imagine' and 'for a sustainable future' regular weight at 10% of slide height, 'glass' bold-looking at about 16% of slide height. Footer text: light grey #BFBFBF Bahnschrift, about 1.5% of slide height. Accents: teal #01A5A5 at 25% opacity in the corner shape and teal #01A5A5 footer bar, light teal #78CFCF for the tab. No rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients on rectangles.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Fix the blank slide 8 and the clipped 'GENDA' title, and enlarge or cut the small text on slides 7, 15, 16, 18, 41, 45 and 47.

**Text**

1 / INTRODUCTION / OUR PURPOSE / A TWO-PRONGED STRATEGY TO DEVELOP OUR PURPOSE / PERFORMANCE OF A MATERIALITY ANALYSIS* / Verallia developed a materiality matrix to reflect the strong identity / of the Group as well as the specific issues relating to its activities / and local presence / It led to the development of three priorities: / 1 / Acting for the safety and development of teams / 2 / Helping to preserve the environment by being / a benchmark player in the circular economy / 3 / Nurturing relationships with our stakeholders / A COLLABORATION WITH ALL VERALLIA’S STAKEHOLDERS / • 42 interviews with external stakeholders / (customers, suppliers, etc.) / • 28 in-depth interviews with internal stakeholders / • Key market focus groups / - 10 employee focus groups were conducted in 5 key markets: / France, Germany, Italy, Iberia, and Latin America / - 1 focus group in each market conducted with Middle / managers & Senior managers / • Global employee consultation / - Online consultation conducted in 13 countries / in 7 languages generating c. 1,500 responses / • 5 webinars with the Divisions to introduce the purpose / • 4 workshops with the Corporate Functions to introduce / the purpose / * For more information, please see the 2019 Statement of Extra-Financial Performance: https://www.verallia.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/Ls9b95-dpef-verallia-2019-en-def.pdf / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 7

**Layout**

Two columns beneath a full-width title block. Title OUR PURPOSE sits top-left at x 2.5 y 4.4 with the grey subtitle directly beneath it. Left column (x 2.7 to 47) holds an intro paragraph and three numbered priorities; right column (x 54.9 to 91) holds a bulleted list with dash sub-bullets. Each column has a centred teal section header flanked by thin horizontal rules at y 24. Left margin about 2.5 percent. Source footnote sits at y 87 above a footer line at y 96.7 with left footer text, a centred logo and page number 7 at bottom right on a teal corner shape.

**Visuals**

Three numbered priority markers in the left column, each a solid teal blob shape (#00B3B0) with a fainter offset teal blob (#00B3B0 at 28 percent opacity) behind it, containing white numerals 1, 2 and 3. A small Verallia logo at bottom centre: a stylised green-teal leaf or drop mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey beside it. No photographs or charts. A decorative pale teal corner shape at bottom right (#01A5A5 at 25 percent opacity) behind the page number. A short teal bar at bottom left under the footer text.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Titles and accents in teal #01A5A5; body text and bullets in mid grey #7F7F7F; tab and small accents in light teal #78CFCF; footer text light grey #BFBFBF; footnote text #A6A6A6. Typeface is Bahnschrift (a DIN-style condensed-leaning humanist sans-serif, Bahnschrift Regular for body, uppercase for titles and headers). Title height about 5.9 percent of slide height; subtitle about 4.4; body about 2.6 to 3.0; footnote and footer about 1.3 to 1.5. Thin teal rules at 1 px width. Teal numeral blobs with soft offset layer; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 9

**Text**

1 / INTRODUCTION / VERALLIA HAS JOINED AS A NETWORK MEMBER / THE ELLEN MACARTHUR FOUNDATION / The Ellen MacArthur Foundation / Launched in 2010 to accelerate the transition to a circular economy / The Foundation works with, and inspires, business, academia, policymakers, / and institutions to mobilise systems solutions at scale, globally / Collaboration / with the Ellen MacArthur Foundation to accelerate progress / across our three priority areas: / accelerating innovation in the glass value chain / encouraging reuse / mobilizing for more recycled glass / Great opportunity for Verallia to work / with leading thinkers, and even more closely with other / network organizations right across our value chain to drive / the transition to a circular economy for glass / New milestone on our journey to make glass / the most sustainable material / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 9

**Layout**

Two-zone free layout on a white field with no column grid: the section tag sits top left, the main title spans the top band starting at about 2.5% from the left edge, and a central text block starts at about 18.6% x. The Ellen MacArthur Member logo sits at left-centre beside a teal blob, and three stacked text blocks step rightward down the right half (Collaboration at x 50%, Great opportunity at x 56.9%, New milestone at x 47.4%), each with a thin vertical teal rule at its left edge. Footer text is bottom left at about 2.8% margin, the Verallia logo is bottom centre, and the page number sits bottom right on a teal corner blob. Text is left aligned throughout.

**Visuals**

1) Ellen MacArthur Foundation Member logo (image, 959 by 270 px, box x 6.8%, y 51.2%, w 39.4%, h 19.8%): black concentric ring mark of three incomplete circles on the left, with the bold black sans-serif wordmark ELLEN MACARTHUR / FOUNDATION in capitals and the word Member in black slab-style serif below. Flat, no tint, not cropped. 2) Verallia logo (image, 256 by 79 px, box x 46.1%, y 94.6%, w 7.8%, h 4.3%): green-teal overlapping leaf-like glossy blobs at left with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey to the right. 3) Large teal organic blob shape at the left edge (box x 0%, y 29.7%, w 18.8%, h 49.4%), fill #01A5A5 at 25% opacity, bleeding off the left edge, rounded irregular outline. 4) Small teal blob in the bottom-right corner (box x 92.6%, y 93.9%, w 7.4%, h 6.1%), fill #01A5A5 at 25% opacity, bleeding off the right and bottom edges. 5) Solid teal bar bottom left under the footer text (about x 2.8%, y 98.9%, w 8.0%, h 0.9%), fill #01A5A5 full opacity. 6) Light teal tab behind the section label (about x 2.7%, y 0%, w 12.0%, h 3.6%), fill #78CFCF, no border. 7) Three thin vertical teal rules, 1 px, #01A5A5: at about x 48.9% y 44% to 58% beside Collaboration, about x 56.1% y 62.5% to 74.5% beside Great opportunity, and about x 46.2% y 77% to 85% beside New milestone.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a 25%-opacity teal #01A5A5 blob at left and bottom right. Titles and accents teal #01A5A5; the section label Introduction is white #FFFFFF on a #78CFCF tab; the slide number is white #FFFFFF; body and subheading text grey #7F7F7F; footer text light grey #BFBFBF. Typeface is Bahnschrift, a DIN-style condensed sans-serif, regular weight for body and headings, with the main title in all capitals. Main title about 5.9% of slide height; section subheading about 4.4%; teal lead lines about 3.3% (headings) and 2.6% (body); grey body about 2.96% and 2.04%. Flat shapes, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded rectangles; rules are plain straight lines.

**Layout**

Four equal columns of section tiles spread across the slide width, each with a blob-framed image, a short horizontal bar, an uppercase section label and a presenter portrait with name and role beneath. The second column is active and in full colour, the others are faded to grey. The title GENDA sits top-left at about 10% of slide width and 6% from the top. Footer caption bottom-left at 2.8% from the left edge, verallia logo centred at the bottom, page number 10 at bottom-right on a teal corner wedge.

**Visuals**

Four blob-framed section images. Column 1 (faded, grey overlay): photograph of glass bottles of varied shapes and jars on a light background, full composition in an organic rounded-triangle frame. Column 2 (full colour, teal ring behind): split photograph, left half shows an amber-orange glass bottle with a warm glowing backlight, right half shows a close-up of a tree trunk with green ivy leaves; a thin white infinity-like curved line crosses the join. Column 3 (faded grey): flat grey line icon of a circular ring with four spokes ending in cog-like knobs, arranged in a loop with gaps. Column 4 (faded grey overlay): photograph of many dark and clear glass bottles and jars standing close together, lit from the front. Three teal-ringed circular portrait photos in the active column, each a greyscale head-and-shoulders headshot with a light grey background: Laetitia FABRE (woman, dark hair pulled back, smiling, light grey top), Romain BARRAL (man, short grey hair, dark jacket, smiling), Denis MICHEL (man, short grey hair, dark suit and open-collar white shirt). Faded grey portraits for Michel GIANNUZZI (column 1, man, bald, smiling, dark jacket), Wendy KOOL-FOULON (column 3, woman, long blonde hair, smiling) and Michel GIANNUZZI (column 4). Portraits are cropped to organic blob shapes with a teal offset ring behind the active ones. Verallia logo: a green-to-teal overlapping circle mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey to its right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with a pale teal corner wedge at bottom-right. Title GENDA in teal #01A5A5 regular-weight DIN-style sans-serif, all capitals, large (about 10% of slide height). Active section label in mid grey #7F7F7F, medium-weight sans-serif capitals at about 3% of slide height; inactive labels in light grey #BFBFBF. Presenter names in teal #01A5A5 (active) or grey #7F7F7F (inactive), medium weight, about 1.7% of slide height; roles in regular weight, about 1.5%. Section bars are short horizontal lines: teal #01A5A5 for the active column, light grey #BFBFBF for the others, about 4% wide and thin. Blob frames: grey #BFBFBF and #E4E6E9 outer rings with white inner cutouts; active blob uses teal #78CFCF and #01A5A5 rings. Translucent white panels at 70% opacity sit behind columns. Footer caption in light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.5% of slide height. Page number white on a teal #01A5A5 wedge at 25% opacity. No borders, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Three equal columns of centred content sit below a two-line uppercase title that starts at the left margin at about 2.5% of the width and runs toward the top right. The section tag sits at the top left and the UN Global Compact logo at the top right. A footer line sits at bottom left, the verallia logo is centred at the bottom, and the page number sits at bottom right on a teal corner shape. Each column holds a blob-shaped photo, a three-line heading and a row of three SDG icons.

**Visuals**

Three photographs in organic rounded-triangle (blob) crops, each about 21.5% of the slide width and 37% of the slide height. Left: close-up of broken green, brown and clear glass cullet piled in a heap, lit evenly, filling the blob. Middle: a wide view of a industrial plant with two tall brown-red chimneys, grey silos and conveyor structures in front of brown hills and a snowy mountain range under a pale sky; a small verallia sign is visible on a building at lower left. Right: a smiling woman with blonde hair in a navy cap and navy and lime-green high-visibility jacket, seated at a table with papers and a pen, three blurred colleagues in grey and navy workwear behind her in a bright white room; the crop is a blob with the top-right edge cutting her cap. Also one UN Global Compact emblem (grey globe with laurel wreath and curved text 'UN GLOBAL COMPACT') at top right, and the verallia logo (green-teal swirl mark with grey-teal wordmark) at bottom centre. Nine UN SDG icons arranged in three groups of three, each a square tile with a number, a label and a white pictogram: left group 12 RESPONSIBLE CONSUMPTION AND PRODUCTION (ochre #B8862B tile with infinity-loop pictogram), 13 CLIMATE ACTION (green #3F7F3F tile with eye-globe pictogram), 17 PARTNERSHIPS FOR THE GOALS (dark blue #1F4E79 tile with interlocking rings pictogram); middle group 13 CLIMATE ACTION (green #3F7F3F tile with eye-globe pictogram), 15 LIFE ON LAND (bright green #5DBB2E tile with tree and bird pictogram), 17 PARTNERSHIPS FOR THE GOALS (dark blue #1F4E79 tile with interlocking rings pictogram); right group 8 DECENT WORK AND ECONOMIC GROWTH (crimson #A0173F tile with bar chart and rising arrow pictogram), 5 GENDER EQUALITY (red-orange #FF3B1F tile with gender symbol pictogram), 17 PARTNERSHIPS FOR THE GOALS (dark blue #1F4E79 tile with interlocking rings pictogram). Photos are not framed, have no border or overlay.

### Slide 12

**Text**

2
CSR STRATEGY
ENHANCE
THE CIRCULARITY
OF GLASS PACKAGING

17 PARTNERSHIPS FOR THE GOALS
Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021
verallia
12

**Layout**

Asymmetric two-zone layout: a large organic blob-shaped photograph of glass fragments occupies the left half, and a text block with a thin vertical rule sits in the right half, with the three SDG icons in a horizontal row beneath the headline. There is no conventional title placeholder; the section tag sits in the top-left corner, the footer sits bottom-left, the Verallia logo sits bottom-centre, and the page number sits bottom-right on a teal corner blob.

**Visuals**

Photograph: close-up of crushed green, brown, clear and white glass fragments heaped across the frame, shot at a slight angle with shallow depth of field and sunlit highlights on the shard edges. The photo is cropped into an irregular rounded teardrop/blob shape (not a rectangle) and is not full-bleed. A translucent teal (#01A5A5 at 25% opacity) shape overlaps the left portion of the photo and extends beyond it to the left edge of the slide. Dominant photo colours: deep green, dark olive, white and amber-brown. Three SDG icons in a row, each a square tile with no border: (1) SDG 12 Responsible Consumption and Production, square in ochre-gold #BF8B30 with white number 12, white text RESPONSIBLE CONSUMPTION AND PRODUCTION and a white infinity-loop arrow symbol; (2) SDG 13 Climate Action, square in green #3F7E44 with white number 13, white text CLIMATE ACTION and a white eye outline containing a globe; (3) SDG 17 Partnerships for the Goals, square in dark blue #1F4E79 with white number 17, white text PARTNERSHIPS FOR THE GOALS and a white interlinked-rings emblem. Verallia logo: teal and green overlapping rounded blobs with the wordmark verallia in dark grey lowercase sans-serif. A thin dark grey vertical rule separates the text from the image. Small teal blob in bottom-right corner behind the page number.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Headline in teal #01A5A5 for the first two lines and grey #7F7F7F for the third line, set in Bahnschrift (DIN-style condensed-width grotesque sans-serif), regular weight, all capitals, with slight letter-spacing. Headline cap height is about 5.2% of slide height. Footer text in light grey #BFBFBF Bahnschrift at about 1.5% of slide height. Section tag text in white Bahnschrift capitals on a #78CFCF teal box; the number 2 is in #78CFCF. Teal accents #01A5A5 at 25% opacity and solid #78CFCF. Shapes are organic and unrounded; no shadows or gradients. Thin vertical line in dark grey.

### Slide 13

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – ENHANCE THE CIRCULARITY OF GLASS PACKAGING / CIRCULARITY IS AT THE HEART / OF OUR MODEL / Extraction and processing / of raw materials / (sand, limestone, soda ash) / Eco-design and manufacturing / of quality glass packaging / Collection and awareness / on sorting and recycling / SAVINGS / +10 / pts / of cullet / -5 / % / of CO2 / emissions / -2.5 / % / of energy / consumption / Processing of collected glass into cullet / via the recycling of glass in cullet treatment centers / (Verallia or partners) / Packing and marketing / enhancement of our customers’ products / and well-being of end consumers / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / verallia / 13

**Layout**

Two-tier header: a small section tag and a large uppercase title sit top-left on a shared left margin of about 2.5%, with the title spanning the top of the slide. A large circular process diagram fills the left and centre (about 2.5% to 71% wide, 12% to 87% tall) with five labelled stage callouts placed around it. A right-hand column (about 82% to 97%) holds the SAVINGS heading and three stacked statistics. Footer text sits bottom-left at about 2.8% from the left edge and 96.7% from the top, the Verallia logo sits bottom-centre, and the slide number 13 sits bottom-right on a teal corner shape.

**Visuals**

One large flat vector illustration in an infinity-loop (figure-eight) process diagram, drawn as a light grey-green looping road band with darker green chevron arrows showing a clockwise flow. Stage 1 (top-left, orange #F9B200): a conical heap of sand and an orange sun-like dot with a leader line, plus three orange-tinted dump trucks driving up the loop. Stage 2 (top-centre, magenta #E81773): an isometric glass factory with a tall chimney and a magenta-outlined silo, and a small magenta truck leaving it; a dotted magenta route line runs down from the factory to the centre. Stage 3 (top-right, teal #01A5A5): a teal recycling bin with a leader dot above, a teal-outlined garbage truck with recycling symbols tipping cullet, a teal bin with a person beside it, and a teal truck on the upper-right arc with a dotted teal route. Stage 4 (bottom-left, navy #225288): an isometric cullet treatment plant with a conveyor and a pile of teal cullet fragments, a teal truck entering a grey tunnel, and a navy leader dot below the label. Stage 5 (bottom-right, red #E5101B): a red-outlined packing and marketing building with a red bottle and a red dot leader, and a red truck on the lower path. Also a teal cloud outline with the text CO2 and a teal lightning bolt outline in the right column. Small thin-line teal V-shaped chevron below +10 pts of cullet. Ink line style, flat fills, no photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background. Clean white background with a teal title system. Title and tag colours: teal #01A5A5 for the main title, light teal #78CFCF for the top section tag, white #FFFFFF text on the tag. Stage headings are coloured by stage: orange #F9B200, magenta #E81773, teal #00A7A7, navy #225288, red #E5101B. Body text is grey #5A5A5A. Footer text is light grey #BFBFBF. Typeface is Bahnschrift (DIN-style condensed grotesque sans-serif) in regular weight throughout. Title is about 5.9% of slide height in capitals; stage headings about 2.2 to 2.6% and body text about 2.0 to 2.2%; large statistics about 7.4% (numbers) and about 8.2% (+10). Thin hairline rules in teal #01A5A5 frame SAVINGS. The tag and the slide-number corner use flat fills with square corners and no shadows, and the bottom-right corner shape is teal #01A5A5 at 25% opacity. A short teal bar sits at the bottom-left under the footer.

14

**Layout**

A full-width header band sits at the top: a small teal numbered tab '2' followed by a section label bar, with the uppercase title left-aligned directly below at about 2.5% from the left edge. A four-column table (goals, performance indicators, 2019, 2025 targets) occupies the upper half from x 2.7% to 97.5%, followed by a centred two-line heading and a row of three numbered teal blobs in equal columns, with the footnote, footer text, centred Verallia logo and page number in the bottom band.

**Visuals**

Table with four columns and three rows (one header row, two data rows): column 1 'OUR GOALS' (cells: 'Enable the increase of cullet collection by 7 pts vs 2019'; 'Increase the rate of use of external cullet by 10 pts vs 2019'); column 2 'PERFORMANCE INDICATORS*' (cells: '% of domestic used glass collected in Verallia’s countries in Europe'; 'Rate of external cullet usage in our glass production sites'); column 3 '2019' (cells: '76%*' and '49%'); column 4 '2025 TARGETS' (cells: '83%' and '59%'). The 2025 targets column has a pale mint tint #E8F7F6 and its values are teal. Three numbered steps: numerals 1, 2, 3 in large teal type, each beside a thin vertical teal divider line and a teal organic blob (solid #00B3B0 with a lighter offset #00B3B0 copy at 28% opacity behind it, slightly up and left). Each blob carries white centred text: 'Take part in joint initiatives to increase cullet collection', 'Improve recycling capacity and efficiency', 'Optimize cullet usage in Group furnaces'. Logo: Verallia logo at bottom centre, a stylised overlapping teal-green drop mark on the left with the lowercase dark wordmark 'verallia' on the right. No photographs. Bottom-right corner has a pale teal curved shape.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with teal and grey palette and a sans-serif typeface resembling Bahnschrift (DIN-style, regular weight). Title is teal #01A5A5 in uppercase, about 5.9% of slide height. Table headers are white uppercase on light teal #78CFCF; table body text is dark grey #5A5A5A at about 2.6% of slide height with teal #01A5A5 highlighted phrases and values. Heading line 'to maximize...' is grey #7F7F7F at about 4.45%, and the bold-looking line '3 main levers for action' is teal #01A5A5 at about 5.9%. Numerals are teal #01A5A5 at about 8.9%. Blob labels are white regular at about 3.3%. Table rules: thick teal bottom rule #00B3B0 about 0.4% high, thin light teal row dividers #78CFCF. Blobs are organic rounded shapes with no outline and no shadow. No gradients.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Fix the blank slide 8 and the clipped 'GENDA' title, and enlarge or cut the small text on slides 7, 15, 16, 18, 41, 45 and 47.

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – ENHANCE THE CIRCULARITY OF GLASS PACKAGING / LEVER #1: / JOINT INITIATIVES TO INCREASE CULLET COLLECTION / CASE STUDY / FEVE PARTNERSHIP AND LOCAL INITIATIVES / WORKING DIRECTLY WITH LOCAL AUTHORITIES / FEVE* / European glass packaging industry to boost EU glass collection / for recycling to 90% by 2030 (from 76% in 2018¹) / CLOSE THE GLASS LOOP PROGRAM AMBITIONS / 1. MORE / Promote selective / glass collection / to increase cullet / quantity & quality / 2. BETTER / Optimize and develop sorting / & treatment systems to / increase yield and generate / more furnace-ready cullet / 3. TOGETHER / Exchange of knowledge and / best practices in collection, / sorting & treatment / systems between countries / Chile / Russia: work with cities around our plants to help / them increase collection (+30% increase in cullet used expected in 2021) / Rancagua / 225 k hab / Chile / (Region VI, / 1 m inhabitants) / FRIENDS OF GLASS – SPANISH INITIATIVE VIA ANFEVI / CHIN CHIN Campaign on social media June to September 2020 / Influencers on Instagram, Tik Tok plus youtube video + 5.6 million views / VOLGOGRAD / SOTCHI / SOUTH CAUCASUS / Russia / (South Caucasus, / 25 m inhabitants) / Cities involved / Verallia Plant / *FEVE: Federation of European manufacturers of glass containers for food and beverage and flacons for perfumery, cosmetics and pharmacy markets / 1. Latest 2018 FEVE public figures / Verallia's ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 15

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: the left column (roughly x 3 to 50%) holds the FEVE partnership block, three numbered pillars, and a Friends of Glass block with two images; the right column (roughly x 53 to 97%) holds the authorities block with a Chile map, a South Caucasus map, two teal bubbles and a legend. Title sits top-left in one line with the grey subtitle CASE STUDY beneath it; a teal section tab with the number 2 sits at the top-left corner. Section headers are centred between horizontal rules. Footnotes are bottom-left, the Verallia footer text bottom-left, the Verallia logo bottom-centre, and the page number 15 bottom-right on a teal corner shape.

**Visuals**

Chile map: a grey regional map of Chile's Region VI shown as light-grey outlined administrative regions with small black region labels (for example NAVIDAD, LITUECHE, LA ESTRELLA, MARCHIGUE, PICHILEMU, PAREDONES, LOLOL, CHEPICA, SANTA CRUZ, SAN FERNANDO, MACHALI); teal dots mark cities involved, and a teal factory icon marks the Rancagua plant; a teal city marker sits on the Rancagua label. South Caucasus map: a schematic outline map of the Black Sea coast and Caucasus region, with the Russian Federation regions in dark grey and Georgian and Armenian areas in white outline; labels VOLGOGRAD and SOTCHI in dark grey bold capitals with black dots; teal dots mark cities involved and teal factory icons mark plants. Ring graphic: a circle of about 150 small dots in green, blue, light-green and pink on a white background, arranged in a loose ring, placed behind the heading CLOSE THE GLASS LOOP PROGRAM AMBITIONS and overlapping its last word. Photograph left: a dark silhouette of a person's head and shoulders against a bright window showing blurred apartment blocks, high contrast, boxed rectangle, not cropped. Photograph centre: two hands clinking beer bottles against a sunset sky with orange-yellow glow, boxed; to its right a cropped screenshot of an Instagram post with small grey text and icons on white. Legend: a teal filled circle labelled Cities involved and a teal factory icon labelled Verallia Plant. Verallia logo: a green and teal three-blob mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with teal and grey corporate styling. Title LEVER #1: JOINT INITIATIVES TO INCREASE CULLET COLLECTION in teal #01A5A5 all-caps regular sans-serif (Bahnschrift-like), about 5.9% of slide height; CASE STUDY in grey #7F7F7F all-caps about 4.4%. Section headers in teal #01A5A5 all-caps about 2.6%. Body text grey #5A5A5A and #7F7F7F sans-serif about 2.0 to 2.2%. Accent figures in teal #01A5A5 (90% by 2030, + 5.6 million views, Chile / Russia:). Pillar headings in teal #00A7A7 about 2.2%. Header tab fill #78CFCF with white text. Chile and Russia bubbles are blob shapes in teal #00B3B0, with a second paler offset blob at 28% opacity behind each, white text. Thin teal horizontal rules bracket section headers. Corner shape bottom-right in teal at 25% opacity. No shadows or gradients.

CSR STRATEGY – ENHANCE THE CIRCULARITY OF GLASS PACKAGING
LEVER #2:
IMPROVE RECYCLING CAPACITY AND EFFICIENCY
CASE STUDY
INVESTMENT IN CULLET TREATMENT CAPACITY
(DIRECT PARTNERSHIPS AND THROUGH EXTERNAL SUPPLIERS)
Verallia – 8 cullet treatment plants
• Invested €15m in its cullet treatment plants (2018-2020)
• Has teamed up with a leading Spanish cullet treatment operator to set up a new cullet treatment plant close to Madrid in 2019
• Bought recycling facility in the Canary Islands in 2018
• Has increased the efficiency of recycling process to
• improve separation of colored and non colored cullet and
• improve cullet re-use rate in non colored glass at its own as well as suppliers’ facilities
• Is treating 50% of its cullet in house
118
Joint Venture with TM Alcudia Reciclatges in Guadalajara
Available glass waste in Madrid: 118 kt
• Increase treatment capacity for Verallia Iberia of +40 kt/year
• Flint cullet production of 10 kt/year
Recicladora de Vidrio de Canarias since October 2018
• In 2019: production of 35 kt of usable cullet
• Treatment capacity/year: 45 kt/year
New Cullet plant – Guadalajara district – 9,300 m² land with 5,000 m² of constructed facilities
Verallia plants
Vidrieras Canarias
Cullet treatment plant – Gran Canarias
Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

The slide is a free layout on a white field with a title block at top left, a centred teal subtitle with a thin teal rule on either side, a map of Spain and Portugal with the Canary Islands inset on the left, a legend row beneath the map, and a text column on the right with a headline, bullet list, a rounded photo and a second text block. The header tag is at top left, the Verallia logo sits centred at the bottom, the footer is at bottom left and the slide number is in a small teal corner shape at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Outline map of Spain and Portugal drawn in thin grey (#7F7F7F-like) lines on white, with the Canary Islands inset in a box at bottom left and a scale bar (150 km / 100 mi) at bottom right of the map, credit d-maps.com. Teal (#01A5A5) factory icons mark Verallia plants at about six locations: northern Spain near Burgos, Basque area near Zaragoza, Catalonia near Barcelona, central Spain near Madrid, Portugal near Lisbon, and Andalusia near Seville, plus one in Gran Canaria. A teal rounded blob labelled 118 sits at the Madrid area beside a teal star marking the new cullet plant near Guadalajara. Light teal factory icons mark Vidrieras Canarias in the Canary Islands. A large light grey (#BFBFBF, 49% opacity) curved arrow sweeps from the Madrid area up and over toward Catalonia and then down-right, ending in an arrowhead. A photograph of glass cullet cascading down a conveyor or chute, with green and white fragments falling over a grey metal drum, shown in a blob-shaped crop with a teal (#01A5A5, 25% opacity) blob behind and a white blob offset behind it. Logo: the Verallia logo, a green and teal stylised glass shape with the word verallia in lowercase grey-green type, bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in teal #01A5A5 (LEVER line and IMPROVE line in #00A4A3 and #01A5A5) in uppercase Bahnschrift regular, title about 5.9% of slide height; the CASE STUDY subtitle is grey #7F7F7F in Bahnschrift regular, about 4.4%. Subtitle lines are teal #01A5A5 uppercase Bahnschrift, about 2.6%. Headline Verallia – 8 cullet treatment plants is teal #01A5A5 Bahnschrift regular at about 3.3%. Body bullets are grey #7F7F7F Bahnschrift regular at about 2.2%, with sub-bullets same size. Sub-headings in the right column are teal #01A5A5 Bahnschrift regular at about 2.2%. Bullet glyphs are teal #01A5A5 ArialMT. Header tag is a flat #78CFCF rectangle with white uppercase Bahnschrift text at about 2.0%; slide number 2 in #78CFCF. Footer text is light grey #BFBFBF Bahnschrift at about 1.5%. A thin teal horizontal rule of #01A5A5 flanks the centred subtitle. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients. Photo has an organic blob crop with a soft teal (#01A5A5 at 25% opacity) offset shape behind it. Bottom-right teal corner shape is #01A5A5 at 25% opacity with the white page number 16 on top. A small teal bar sits at bottom left under the footer in #78CFCF.

**Layout**

Full-bleed white slide with a teal section tab in the top-left corner and a large uppercase title running across the top at about a 2.5% left margin, with the title baseline near 10% of the height. The body is a three-column arrangement: a left text column centred near x 17%, a central cluster of three photographs with a teal blob backdrop spanning about x 33% to 59%, and a right text column centred near x 83%, with a central teal '2 drivers' badge above the cluster and two grey curved arrows linking it to the columns. The footer sits bottom-left, the Verallia logo is bottom-centre, and the slide number is bottom-right on a translucent teal corner blob.

**Visuals**

Three photographs, one large and two small, plus decorative blob shapes, two grey curved arrows, a teal '2 drivers' badge and the Verallia logo. The large photo (about x 41.5%, y 42.5%, w 25.9%, h 44.6%, 691 by 668 px) is cropped to an irregular organic blob: a worker in a grey protective sleeve reaches into industrial glass-forming machinery, with metal fixtures and a warm orange-brown interior; framing is medium, lighting is warm and industrial, dominant colours are rust brown, orange and grey, no overlay. The upper-left small photo (about x 33.3%, y 46.5%, w 14.6%, h 25.1%, 389 by 376 px) is a blob with a thin white outline showing bright orange molten glass flowing in a channel, lit intensely, dominant colours orange and deep red. The lower-right small photo (about x 59.4%, y 73.8%, w 10.4%, h 17.9%, 276 by 267 px) is a blob with a thin white outline showing rows of amber glass bottles on a dark background, lit warm orange, dominant colours amber and black. Behind the photos are pale teal blob shapes (#00B3B0 at 28% opacity). The teal badge at about x 44%, y 19%, w 12.5%, h 23.8% is a solid #00B3B0 organic blob with a lighter #00B3B0 blob at 28% opacity offset behind it at about x 43.9%, y 18.4%, w 14.5%, h 23.8%. Two light grey (about #D4D4D4) curved arrows with rounded ends: the left one runs from about x 24%, y 24% to x 44%, y 38%, ending with an arrowhead pointing down-left; the right one runs from about x 59%, y 25% to x 77%, y 38%, ending with an arrowhead pointing down-right. The Verallia logo at about x 46.1%, y 94.6%, w 7.8%, h 4.3% is a teal circular leaf-like icon with overlapping green-teal rounded forms beside the lowercase wordmark 'verallia' in dark grey-teal.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and headings are teal (#01A5A5 for the main title and column headings, #00A4A3 for the 'LEVER #3:' fragment); body text is mid grey #7F7F7F; the section tab is light teal #78CFCF with white text and the section number '2' in #78CFCF; the footer is light grey #BFBFBF; the slide number is white. Typeface is Bahnschrift, a DIN-style sans-serif with regular weight, uppercase for the title and column headings, and sentence case for body text. Title is about 5.9% of slide height, column headings about 3.0%, body about 3.0%, the badge numeral about 7.4% and 'drivers' about 4.4%, footer about 1.5%. Body and headings are centre-aligned. Shapes are organic blobs with no outlines, no shadows and no corner radii other than the blob curvature; photographs have a thin white edge on the two small images; the arrows are soft grey strokes of about 1% of slide height thickness with rounded ends. Accent bar at bottom-left is #78CFCF.

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – ENHANCE THE CIRCULARITY OF GLASS PACKAGING / GOING ONE STEP FURTHER: / EXPAND REUSE BUSINESS MODELS WHERE IT MAKES SENSE / 2025 TARGETS / At least 1 pilot in France / REUSE: re-imagine an heritage on the impulse of zero waste, bulk and short-circuit trends / Returnable Glass Bottles is both a trend that supports sustainability and an opportunity for extra business / REUSE: A LONGSTANDING FEATURE / OF THE GLASS INDUSTRY / RENEWED EFFORT TO PROACTIVELY DRIVE REUSE / IN NEW GEOGRAPHIES / GERMANY / Returnable Glass Bottles (RGB): / Market share of different mineral water container (%) / 37.8 / 34.6 / 29.9 / 35.3 / 23.7 / 22.5 / YTD June 2019 / YTD June 2020 / Reusable PET / Disposable PET / Reusable Glass / Source: Lebensmittelzeitung / A new 2020 Reusable retro-look 33cl Verallia exclusive / design. Not customer-exclusive development. / Today more than 10 breweries have decided to use it. / Objective: 30 millions of bottles in 2021. / Pictures from Veltins, Krombacher and Bitburger / FRANCE: first steps of new initiative / 6 ongoing product developments / to proactively meet the objective / of a standard reusable glass range / in France by 2022 (AGEC law) / More than 5 meetings with players / interested in launching reusable / bottles/jars / Verallia France invited as a speaker / during the “Rencontres Techniques / de la Consigne” / >> / Special awards given to 2 students / for the design of rosé wine reusable / bottles / 2020 VERALLIA DESIGN AWARDS / TERRASSE ROSÉE / Sodastream / SodaStream Design / Registration No. 006364246 / Co-design / of DUO 1L bottle / matching carbonation / and reuse / Pülleken / LIMO BIER / Benediktiner Weiss / verallia / 18 / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a section tag bar sits top-left, a large two-line title block (teal headline over grey subtitle) spans the top-left, then a full-width 2025 TARGETS band with a centred target line and a thick teal rule beneath, followed by two centred-heading columns. The left column (about 3% to 38% of width) holds the German bar chart with a caption and beer-bottle photo; the right column (about 40% to 97%) holds the French bullets, award and magazine images, and the Sodastream product block. Margins are about 3% left and right. Footer text sits bottom-left, the Verallia logo bottom-centre, and the page number 18 bottom-right on a teal corner shape.

**Visuals**

1) Bar chart, titled GERMANY Returnable Glass Bottles (RGB): Market share of different mineral water container (%). Vertical bar chart, three groups on the category axis labelled Reusable PET, Disposable PET, Reusable Glass; no numeric axis is printed, so bar heights are estimates in percent of the chart height. Series 1 (grey #BFBFBF, YTD June 2019): Reusable PET 23.7, Disposable PET 37.8, Reusable Glass 29.9. Series 2 (light teal #78CFCF, YTD June 2020): Reusable PET 22.5, Disposable PET 34.6, Reusable Glass 35.3 printed in dark teal #0098AF to highlight it. Legend at right of chart: grey dot YTD June 2019, teal dot YTD June 2020. Three teal hand-drawn trend arrows: down-right arrow above Reusable PET, down-right arrow above Disposable PET, up-right arrow above Reusable Glass. Source line Lebensmittelzeitung below the axis. 2) Photo of three beer bottles with paper labels (about 2 of 3 visible labels legible as Pülleken, LIMO BIER and Benediktiner Weiss), standing upright on white, boxed, no crop, neutral daylight. 3) German flag icon, a circular glossy badge with black, red and gold horizontal stripes. 4) French flag icon, a circular glossy badge with blue, white and red vertical stripes. 5) Image of the 2020 VERALLIA DESIGN AWARDS graphic, large pale '2020' numerals behind the wordmark, on white. 6) Magazine spread image (Terrasse Rosée, a page with photographs of wicker chair, interior and outdoor scenes, and dense small text) placed left of a grey-toned studio photo of two rosé-tinted reusable wine bottles with textured glass, soft diffuse light, cropped as one rectangle. 7) Product photo of a SodaStream DUO 1L glass carafe bottle with black cap, filled with sparkling water, on white, full height of its box. 8) Verallia logo: a green-teal stylised leaf/bubble mark with lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey, bottom centre. 9) Decorative leaf-shaped teal blob at 25% opacity behind the French bullet text and magazine images.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Section tag bar #78CFCF with white uppercase text. Main title in teal #01A5A5, semibold sans-serif uppercase (Bahnschrift-style DIN-like grotesque), about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in grey #7F7F7F regular uppercase, about 4.4%. Body text grey #7F7F7F regular, about 1.8% to 2.0%. Section headings in teal #01A5A5 uppercase, about 2.6%, flanked by thin teal horizontal rules. Accent colours: teal #01A5A5, light teal #78CFCF, dark teal #0098AF, and a grey #5A5A5A line of text. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; rectangles have square corners. The 2025 TARGETS band is a solid light-teal rectangle. A thick teal horizontal rule (about 0.4% height) sits under the target line. Footer grey #BFBFBF. Teal corner shape at 25% opacity bottom-right with rounded inner edge.

### Slide 19

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY / SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE / OUR CO2 EMISSIONS / ACROSS OUR OPERATIONS / 13 CLIMATE ACTION / 15 LIFE ON LAND / 17 PARTNERSHIPS FOR THE GOALS / verallia / 19 / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white slide: a large organic blob-shaped photograph occupies the left half (about x 7.7 to 54 percent, y 18 to 92 percent), and the right half holds a headline block with a thin vertical grey rule at about x 57 percent, followed by a row of three SDG tiles below it. The section tag CSR STRATEGY sits in the top-left corner, flush to the top edge, with the slide number 2 to its left. The footer line sits bottom-left at about x 2.8 percent, the Verallia logo is centred at the bottom, and the page number 19 sits bottom-right on a small pale teal corner arc. Left-aligned text, generous white space above the headline.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a wide, eye-level view of an industrial plant (appears to be a glass or lime production site) on dry, scrubby hills. Two tall brick-red chimneys rise from the centre, flanked by beige and cream silos, a grey pipe run, a yellow conveyor gantry, and low beige and tan buildings; one building carries the Verallia logo. Behind the plant are green tree rows at left, brown-ochre hills, a line of wind turbines, and snow-capped mountains on the horizon under a pale grey-blue sky. Lighting is soft, even overcast daylight with muted, desaturated tones. Dominant colours are grey-blue sky, ochre and olive hills, beige buildings and brick red chimneys. The photo is cropped into an irregular rounded blob shape with no border. A translucent teal blob (about 25 percent opacity, #01A5A5) overlaps the left edge of the photo. Three SDG icon tiles: 13 Climate Action (white eye shape containing a globe on dark green #3F7E44 square); 15 Life on Land (white tree with birds and ground lines on bright green #56C02B square); 17 Partnerships for the Goals (white interlocking rings on navy #19486A square). Each tile is square, no rounded corners, no shadow. Small Verallia logo on the building in the photo and a Verallia logo centred in the footer (teal-green swirl mark with wordmark in dark grey).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Headline in uppercase Bahnschrift-style condensed/semi-condensed geometric sans-serif, regular weight, with letter-spacing; first two lines in teal #01A5A5 and the last line in mid grey #7F7F7F. Headline cap height about 5.2 percent of slide height (font size about 5.18 percent). Footer and small text in light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.5 percent of slide height. Section tag text white #FFFFFF on teal #78CFCF-filled box, about 2.0 percent. Accent colours: teal #01A5A5 and pale teal #78CFCF; pale teal corner arc #01A5A5 at 25 percent opacity. Blob shapes are organic, soft-edged; no gradients or shadows. Thin dark grey vertical rule about 0.1 percent wide. Teal bar at bottom-left, about 6.1 percent tall in the footer strip.

### Slide 20

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO2 EMISSIONS ACROSS OPERATIONS / CO2 EMISSIONS: THE 3 SCOPES / HFCs / N2O / CH4 / CO2 / SCOPE 2 / INDIRECT / SCOPE 3 / INDIRECT / SCOPE 1 / DIRECT / SCOPE 3 / INDIRECT / PFCs / SF6 / leased / assets / Company / facilities / electricity / for own use / employee / communiting / transformation / & distribution / business / travel / processing of / sold products / UPSTREAM ACTIVITIES / REPORTING COMPANY / DOWNSTREAM ACTIVITIES / Purchased goods / & sevices / fuel & energy / related / fuel & energy / related / transformation / & distribution / waste from / operations / use of sold / products / end-of-life / treatment of / sold products / leased / assets / franchises / investments / *For Verallia emissions from GHG other than CO2 are insignificant / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / verallia / 20

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a teal section tag bar running across the top-left (header strip from about 2.7% to 51% of the width, top edge at 0%) carrying the section number 2 and the uppercase header line; the title CO2 EMISSIONS: THE 3 SCOPES sits left-aligned just below it at about 4.4% from the top. A large central diagram image fills the middle band from about 15.6% to 90.4% of the height, spanning the full width. Below it a row of three chevron-tipped bands (upstream, reporting company, downstream) sits at about 85% to 88.8% height, with a small italic footnote right-aligned at about 91% and a footer row at about 96.5% with the Verallia logo centred and the page number 20 in the bottom-right corner on a light teal quarter-circle.

**Visuals**

One diagram, a raster illustration of a greenhouse-gas scope model. Subject: three upward arrows rising from a bottom ribbon of activity icons, set against stacked grey clouds labelled CO2, CH4, N2O, HFCs, PFCs and SF6. Arrows: a green arrow on the left labelled SCOPE 2 INDIRECT (about x 32 to 41%, y 50 to 72%); a teal arrow left of centre labelled SCOPE 3 INDIRECT (about x 38 to 46%, y 46 to 71%); a dark blue to violet arrow in the centre labelled SCOPE 1 DIRECT (about x 44 to 56%, y 43 to 82%); a teal arrow right of centre labelled SCOPE 3 INDIRECT (about x 53 to 62%, y 46 to 71%). Inside the arrows are icons with labels: lightning bolt in circle with electricity for own use; stacked-drawer icon with leased assets; train with employee communiting; airplane with business travel; stacked-drawer icon with Company facilities; ship with transformation & distribution (upper right); factory with processing of sold products; battery with use of sold products; recycling bin with end-of-life treatment of sold products; pallet stack with leased assets; storefront with franchises; money bag with investments; shopping cart with Purchased goods & sevices; truck with fuel & energy related; drop in bottle with fuel & energy related; ship with transformation & distribution (lower left); waste bin and cup icon with waste from operations. A grey ring-shaped arc links the clouds behind the arrows. Ribbon of icons sits on a rounded-corner rectangle (about x 13.9 to 86.1%, y 70.9 to 81.6%). The image is not cropped; it is a rectangular box with a soft grey gradient background (lighter at the centre top, darker grey at the edges). Lighting is flat and even; no photograph. Dominant colours: lime green #A3CD39, teal #1BAFC6, blue #1E7BC4, violet #6B5FBF at the base of the centre arrow, cloud greys #B9BEC6 to #E2E4E8. The three bands below: UPSTREAM ACTIVITIES, a teal to blue arrow-tipped bar (about x 13.9 to 48.5%, y 85.2 to 88.8%, fill #1AA6B5); REPORTING COMPANY, a deeper blue arrow-tipped bar (about x 41.9 to 56.6%, fill #2A6DB5); DOWNSTREAM ACTIVITIES, a blue arrow-tipped bar (about x 58 to 86.1%, fill #1A7FC0). Verallia logo: a green-teal and blue overlapping mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey, boxed, no tint. A small teal bar sits bottom-left at about x 2.8 to 12.2%, y 98.6 to 99.4%. A 25% opacity teal quarter-circle sits bottom-right behind the page number.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with the diagram image's soft grey gradient occupying the central band. White background. Title and header in teal (#01A5A5), header tag bar fill #78CFCF with white uppercase text, section number in #78CFCF. Body and diagram labels white on the coloured bands; footer text light grey #BFBFBF; footnote italic grey #7F7F7F. Typeface: Bahnschrift (DIN-style condensed-width sans-serif, regular weight for header and labels, semibold for the title). Title size about 5.9% of slide height, header about 2.0%, diagram labels about 1.1%, footer about 1.5%. Shapes have sharp corners except the rounded-corner bottom band and the quarter-circle page-number shape; no shadows; no outlines. Cloud shapes have soft grey gradients and drop-shadow-like edges; arrows have flat-to-gradient fills without outlines.

### Slide 21

**Text**

2
CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO2 EMISSIONS ACROSS OUR OPERATIONS
CO2 EMISSIONS: 2020 KEY STEPS AND OBJECTIVES
In our 2019 Extra-Financial statement, CO2 reduction target from 2019 to 2030
= -20% ton of CO2 (scopes 1 and 2) per ton of packed glass
2020 > Challenge of our initial target
To support the worldwide initiative
to limit the increase of global
temperature to well-below 2°C
above pre-industrial levels
1
MEASURE THE 2019 CARBON
BALANCE
Scopes 1, 2, 3 (tons)
Scope 1 | 2,479,512 | 50.6%
Scope 2 market-based(1) | 610,653 | 12.5%
Scope 3 | 1,809,918 | 36.9%
Total Scopes 1, 2, 3 | 4,900,083 | -
Our Scope 3 represents less
than 40% of our total Group
emissions
(1) For Scope 2 emissions, “Market-based” will be the calculation method used
Market-based method = based on GHG emissions emitted by the generators from which Verallia contractually purchases electricity bundled with contractual instruments
Location-based method was used until 2019 = based on average energy generation emission factors for defined geographic locations
2019 Scope 2 emissions “location-based” = 487,825 tons, “market-based” = 610,653 tons.
Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021
verallia
21

**Layout**

Header tag band sits top-left above the title, with the title running across the top of the slide under it. Below, a centred two-line statement sits above a three-column arrangement: a left text column, a vertical teal divider with chevrons, and a right-hand table with a callout to its right. Footnotes sit bottom-left, the Verallia logo bottom-centre and the slide number bottom-right, with a small teal bar bottom-left and a teal corner shape bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Table: title 'Scopes 1, 2, 3 (tons)' above a table with three columns (label, tons, share) and four rows. Row 1 Scope 1 | 2,479,512 | 50.6%. Row 2 Scope 2 market-based(1) | 610,653 | 12.5%. Row 3 Scope 3 | 1,809,918 | 36.9%. Row 4 Total Scopes 1, 2, 3 | 4,900,083 | - (dash). Table has a thick teal rule (about #00A4A3, about 0.5% of height) above the header row, thin light-teal row dividers (about #67C8C5 at low weight) and vertical dividers between columns. Row 4 is filled #67C8C5 with white text. Numeric values are shown as printed; no chart. Decorative: a teal numbered blob marking step 1 (large #00B3B0 blob with a lighter #00B3B0 blob at 28% opacity offset behind it, the number 1 in white). Thin grey horizontal rules flank the top statement (left and right, about #7F7F7F). A thin vertical teal line (about #78CFCF) runs from about y 33% to y 94% at about x 32.3%. Two thin teal chevrons: one left-pointing chevron at about x 30.6% to 33.3%, y 50% to 63%, and one right-pointing chevron at about x 73% to 75%, y 64% to 71%. Verallia logo: a green-teal abstract droplet mark with the word verallia in lowercase grey-teal, placed bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with teal accent palette. Titles in teal #01A5A5 / #00A4A3, all caps, Bahnschrift-style condensed DIN-like sans-serif, regular weight, title size about 5.9% of slide height. Body text about 2.6% of slide height, Bahnschrift regular, grey #7F7F7F for statements and table labels, with teal #009999 / #01A5A5 for emphasised call-out lines. Header tag: white caps text on a #78CFCF band, about 2.0% height. Footnotes: italic grey #808080 at about 1.3% height. Footer text #BFBFBF at about 1.5%. Slide number white about 2.2% on teal. Shapes: flat, no shadows, no gradients, square corners on the table and tag band, organic rounded blobs for the step marker. Thin hairline rules about 0.1% thick.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the agenda tracker with presenter photos, the goal-and-indicator table header used on slides 14, 22, 29, 32 and 35, the waterfall on slide 26 with its single takeaway box, and the lever 1/2/3 structure on slides 23 to 25.

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO2 EMISSIONS ACROSS OPERATIONS / CO2 EMISSIONS: 2020 KEY STEPS AND OBJECTIVES / OUR GOAL / PERFORMANCE INDICATORS / 2019 / 2025 TARGETS / 2030 TARGETS / Reduce our absolute CO2 emissions by 27.5% / by 2030 vs 2019, in line with Science-Based Targets / Tons of CO2 emitted (scopes 1 & 2)(1) / 3,090 k / 2,626 k / 2,240 k / 2 / REDACT / AND DISCLOSE / CDP / DISCLOSURE INSIGHT ACTION / A- / 1st Rating 08.12.2020 / 3 / DEFINE THE GHG EMISSIONS / REDUCTION TARGETS / SCIENCE BASED TARGETS / DRIVING AMBITIOUS CORPORATE CLIMATE ACTION / “COMMITTED” status / -27.5% in 2030 / of scopes 1 & 2 emissions / in absolute data from a base year 2019 / well below 2°C trajectory / 4 / ON-GOING 2021: / ACTIVATE SCOPE 3 / REDUCTION LEVERS / • Strategic suppliers / • Logistic optimization / • Lightweight of bottles and jars / (1) Commitment on Scopes 1 & 2, as our Scope 3 emissions represent less than 40% of our total Group emissions (Scopes 1, 2 and 3) / Note: Historical data are shown in Appendices / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / verallia / 22

**Layout**

Single 16:9 slide on a white field: a section label bar sits top-left with the title directly beneath it at about 2.5% left margin, then a full-width table band from about 17% to 28.6% of height. Three numbered columns (steps 2, 3 and 4) sit below, separated by thin vertical rules and chevrons, with footnotes at about 88% to 90% height, a footer line at 96.7% at left, the verallia logo centred at bottom and the page number 22 at bottom right over a teal corner shape.

**Visuals**

Table with five columns and two rows. Header row (teal cells): OUR GOAL | PERFORMANCE INDICATORS | 2019 | 2025 TARGETS | 2030 TARGETS. Body row: Reduce our absolute CO2 emissions by 27.5% by 2030 vs 2019, in line with Science-Based Targets | Tons of CO2 emitted (scopes 1 & 2)(1) | 3,090 k | 2,626 k | 2,240 k; the 2025 and 2030 cells have a pale mint fill and teal values. Three numbered teal organic blob shapes with white numerals 2, 3 and 4, each with a lighter offset copy behind it. Logo of CDP (Disclosure Insight Action): crimson and red stylised geometric mark beside bold red CDP wordmark, with small red caps tagline DISCLOSURE INSIGHT ACTION beneath. Logo of Science Based Targets: thin circular ring of multiple coloured curved strokes (navy, teal, red, orange) forming a wave, with dark grey caps wordmark SCIENCE BASED TARGETS and small tagline DRIVING AMBITIOUS CORPORATE CLIMATE ACTION beneath. Verallia logo: overlapping teal-green leaf or drop shapes with a lowercase dark teal-grey verallia wordmark. Two thin light-teal vertical rules and two outlined chevron arrows (about 1px light teal stroke) divide the three columns. Large grade A- in teal, no chart. A small teal bar sits at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, flat with no gradients or shadows. Section label bar in light teal #78CFCF with white uppercase text; title and grade in teal #01A5A5; body text in mid grey #7F7F7F; step headings and labels in teal #00A7A7; accents #00B3B0 blobs; footer and footnote greys #BFBFBF and #808080. Typeface Bahnschrift (DIN-style humanist grotesque sans-serif), regular weight for body and headings, semi-bold feel on the big figures. Title cap height about 5.9% of slide height; table and body text about 2.2% to 3.3%; footnotes about 1.3%. Blobs are organic rounded shapes with no hard corners; table has square cells; chevrons and dividers are thin hairlines.

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Three numbered levers are each paired with a supporting line chart on the same slide, so the reader sees the action and its trend together.
- Review: Visual identity is coherent and polished, and slides 26 and 23 show strong chart craft with readable headline numbers.
- Worth keeping: Keep the agenda tracker with presenter photos, the goal-and-indicator table header used on slides 14, 22, 29, 32 and 35, the waterfall on slide 26 with its single takeaway box, and the lever 1/2/3 structure on slides 23 to 25.

**Layout**

Single-slide content on a white field with the title running across the top in one line at left margin (about 2.5% from left). Below it a two-column split: the left column (about 3% to 43% wide) holds the LEVER 1 marker and the EXTERNAL CULLET USAGE RATE line chart; the right column (about 46% to 90%) holds the section heading SHIFT RAW MATERIAL MIX with two bulleted subsections. A section tag bar sits in the top-left corner above the title, the Verallia logo is centred at the bottom, the footer text sits bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right on a corner shape.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled EXTERNAL CULLET USAGE RATE. Type: line chart with smooth solid teal line from 2015 to 2020 and a dashed teal segment from 2020 to 2025. Y axis: percent, range 40% to 65%, labelled at 40%, 45%, 50%, 55%, 60%, 65%. X axis: years 2015, 2016, 2017, 2018, 2019, 2020, 2025 (2025 is spaced as the next category after 2020 and is not a true year step). Series: one series, no legend. Labelled values: none printed; estimates: 2015 about 48%, 2016 about 49%, 2017 about 49%, 2018 about 47.5%, 2019 about 49.5%, 2020 about 51%, 2025 about 60%. Highlighted points: small filled teal dots at each data point, the 2025 point is at about 60% and is the endpoint of the dashed projection. Axis baseline is a thin light grey horizontal line at 40%, no gridlines. Also a thick light grey curved arrow pointing from the right column down-left to the chart title. Also a teal blob with the number 1 marking Lever 1. Also the Verallia logo: a teal and green leaf-like emblem with a grey lowercase wordmark verallia.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and accents in teal #01A5A5, with the header tag in light teal #78CFCF and blob shapes in #00B3B0. Body text is medium grey #7F7F7F, footer grey #BFBFBF, chart axis labels grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is Bahnschrift (DIN-style condensed sans-serif) in regular weight throughout, with bullets in ArialMT. Title is about 5.9% of slide height in caps; section heading about 5.2%; subheadings about 3.3%; body about 2.6%; footer about 1.5%. Shapes are flat with no outlines or shadows; the header bar is a sharp rectangle, the number blob is an irregular rounded blob, the bottom-right corner shape is a quarter circle at 25% opacity, the grey arrow is at 49% opacity.

2
CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO2 EMISSIONS ACROSS OUR OPERATIONS
CO2: 3 MAIN LEVERS TO REACH 27.5% REDUCTION BY 2030
LEVER
2
MELTING ENERGY MIX
100%
80%
60%
