20%
0%
2015
2019
2020
2025
Fuel
Nat. Gas
Electricity
Biogas
REDUCE ENERGY CONSUMPTION
Melting energy reduction (CA 80% of total energy consumed)
•  Upgrade of furnace technology (hardware and process control) to address losses, eliminate water ingress via cullet and add pre-heaters
•  Gradual reduction in share of fossil energy used in furnaces, eliminating fuel (highest CO2 generator) and maximizing electrical heating
•  Participation into FEVE “Furnace of the Future” project for the development of next generation furnace using 80% of electrical heating
•  Evaluation of hydrogen usage impact on furnace performance and design through real life test and participation to a consortium led by DNV-GL
Non melting energy reduction (CA 20% of total energy consumed)
•  Address loss reduction on forehearth/heat treatment equipment, optimize supporting fluid generation or heat reuse
Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout on a white field with a full-width-style title band at top: a teal header strip spans the top-left (about 2.7% to 51% of width, 0% to 3.6% of height) carrying a small white running header in caps, and the main title sits below it at the left margin (x 2.5%) at about 4.4% from the top. Below the title, a two-column arrangement: the left column (x 3% to 37%) holds the LEVER 2 badge and a stacked column chart; the right column (x 46% to 94%) holds the section heading REDUCE ENERGY CONSUMPTION at about 24% from the top, followed by two left-aligned subheads with bulleted bodies. A Verallia logo sits centred in the lower band (x 46%, y 94.6%), the footer text sits bottom-left at x 2.8%, y 96.7%, and the page number 24 sits bottom-right at x 95.9%, y 96.8% over a light teal corner shape. Left margin about 2.5% of width; right text edge near 98%.

**Visuals**

1) Stacked column chart titled MELTING ENERGY MIX (teal caps, centred above the chart at about x 18.6%, y 37.7%). Type: stacked column, four columns for 2015, 2019, 2020 and 2025. Y axis: 0% to 100%, ticks every 20% (0%, 20%, 40%, 60%, 80%, 100%), no axis title, no gridlines, light grey baseline. X axis: years 2015, 2019, 2020, 2025, no axis title. Series, bottom to top: Fuel (dark teal #01A5A5) about 27% in 2015, about 10% in 2019, about 9% in 2020, none visible in 2025; Nat. Gas (mid teal #78CFCE) to about 95% in 2015 (about 68% of the column), to about 93% in 2019 (about 83%), to about 93% in 2020 (about 84%), to about 91% in 2025 (about 91%); Electricity (pale teal #CCE8E9) on top, about 5% in 2015, about 7% in 2019, about 7% in 2020, about 6% in 2025 with column tops at about 100%, 100%, 100% and 97.5%. No printed data labels. Legend below the axis, centred at y 77.4%: square swatches for Fuel (dark teal), Nat. Gas (mid teal, its label sits on a light grey #BFBFBF box at about 49% opacity), Electricity (pale teal); Biogas has a label but no visible swatch. No highlighted points. 2) Grey curved arrow (light grey #BFBFBF at about 49% opacity, stroke about 1% of slide height with rounded ends) starting near the right text heading at about x 45%, y 29% and sweeping up and left to an arrowhead pointing down-left at the top of the chart near x 33%, y 40%. 3) LEVER 2 badge: a solid teal #00B3B0 organic blob shape (about 8.3% by 15.8%) with a larger pale teal #00B3B0 blob at about 28% opacity offset behind it, containing a large white numeral 2 in a condensed sans. 4) Small teal numeral 2 beside the header strip and a teal numeral 2 near the title as a subscript in CO2. 5) Verallia logo: a stylised teal-green leaf or drop emblem made of overlapping translucent green and teal rounded forms, followed by the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey, at about x 46%, y 94.6%, w 7.8%, h 4.3%. 6) Decorative shapes: a bottom-left solid teal bar (#78CFCF) about 9% wide and 1% tall at the bottom edge, and a bottom-right pale teal quarter-round shape (#01A5A5 at 25% opacity) at about x 92.6%, y 93.9%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with teal accents. Title in all caps, teal #01A5A5, regular-weight humanist sans (Bahnschrift or close DIN-style sans-serif), about 5.9% of slide height. Header text white caps on a #78CFCF teal strip, about 2.0% of height. Section heading REDUCE ENERGY CONSUMPTION in teal #01A5A5, all caps, about 5.2% of height. Subheads in teal #01A5A5, medium-weight sans, about 3.3% of height, with the parenthetical part in regular weight. Body bullets in mid grey #7F7F7F, regular sans, about 2.6% of height, with teal #01A5A5 round bullets. Chart labels grey #7F7F7F, about 1.7% to 1.8% of height. Footer in light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.5% of height. Page number white, about 2.2% of height. Accents: teal #00B3B0 blob for the lever badge, #78CFCF header strip, #CCE8E9 and #78CFCE chart tints. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; the lever blob is organic with soft rounded contours. Thin teal rule under the word LEVER at about y 28%.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the agenda tracker with presenter photos, the goal-and-indicator table header used on slides 14, 22, 29, 32 and 35, the waterfall on slide 26 with its single takeaway box, and the lever 1/2/3 structure on slides 23 to 25.

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO2 EMISSIONS ACROSS OUR OPERATIONS / CO2: 3 MAIN LEVERS TO REACH 27.5% REDUCTION BY 2030 / LEVER / 3 / Covered ground / 12.000 m2 / Covered warehouses / 36.400 m2 / INCREASE USE OF GREEN ENERGY / Green Energy purchases / • Maximize green energy purchases through certified sources of electricity / (target >60% by 2025, from 34% in 2020) / • Benefit from government-led actions to reduce CO2 emissions / (e.g electricity emission factor reduction or hydrogen introduction / in natural gas) / Biogas utilization / • Replace natural gas with biogases in selected plants / • First pilot under evaluation in Germany with the target to have / 3 furnaces fully supplied by bio-gases by 2030 / On-site energy production / • Verallia to implement on-site renewable electricity production / (e.g solar panels - first project Portugal 2022) / Solar panels installed on roofs and land in Verallia / Portugal plant will start production in 2022 / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 25

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a section tag bar runs across the top-left, with a full-width title below it. The left column (about 5% to 47% of width) holds the LEVER 3 badge, a grey blob backdrop, the site plan and aerial photo side by side, and a caption. The right column (from about 46% to 96%) holds a large heading and three teal subheadings each followed by grey bullets, all left-aligned to a common left edge at about 46%. The footer line sits bottom-left, the Verallia logo bottom-centre-left, and the page number bottom-right in a corner shape. Margins are about 3% left and 4% top.

**Visuals**

Site plan drawing (left, x 6.1 to 28.3 percent, y 37.4 to 69.7 percent): a black-and-white technical plan of an industrial site with outlined building footprints and yellow and grey edges; blue filled rectangles mark solar panel arrays, several in the upper-left and centre, a row of blue panels at lower centre and a larger blue array at lower left. Two red leader arrows with labels 'Covered ground 12.000 m2' (pointing to the upper-right array) and 'Covered warehouses 36.400 m2' (pointing to the lower-centre blue arrays) are drawn in red text on white. Aerial photo (x 28.6 to 41.6 percent, y 39.6 to 69.0 percent): a colour satellite view of a plant with several white-roofed warehouses partly covered by solar panels, surrounded by green trees, roads and red-roofed houses; soft daylight, top-down framing, boxed with square corners and no border. Grey curved arrow (lower centre, roughly x 26 to 46 percent, y 68 to 95 percent): a thick light-grey arc with an arrowhead pointing up-left, sitting behind the lower part of the grey blob. Verallia logo (x 46.1 to 53.9 percent, y 94.6 to 98.9 percent): a teal and green stylised leaf-like mark with the lowercase wordmark 'verallia' in dark grey. Grey blob: a large pale-grey organic leaf-shaped backdrop (#E9EAED) behind the left column. Badge: a teal rounded blob (#00B3B0 with a lighter translucent halo) holding the white numeral 3 next to the word LEVER.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Titles in teal #01A5A5, uppercase, regular-weight humanist sans (Bahnschrift-style, condensed grotesque). Section tag bar in light teal #78CFCF with white uppercase text. Subheadings in teal #01A5A5 at title-case, regular weight. Body bullets in mid-grey #7F7F7F, regular weight, with teal bullet dots. Main heading size about 5.2% of slide height, subheadings about 3.3%, body about 2.6%, header tag about 2.0%, footer about 1.5%. Shapes: organic blobs with no outlines, no shadows, no gradients. Accent: translucent teal rounded shape bottom-right at 25% opacity behind page number. Footer rule: a short teal bar at bottom-left (#78CFCF-like).

### Slide 26

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A waterfall chart breaks the 2019-to-2030 CO2 reduction into raw materials, energy efficiency and greener energy, making the path to the net target easy to follow.
- Review: Visual identity is coherent and polished, and slides 26 and 23 show strong chart craft with readable headline numbers.
- Worth keeping: Keep the agenda tracker with presenter photos, the goal-and-indicator table header used on slides 14, 22, 29, 32 and 35, the waterfall on slide 26 with its single takeaway box, and the lever 1/2/3 structure on slides 23 to 25.

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO₂ EMISSIONS ACROSS OUR OPERATIONS / CO₂ EMISSIONS REDUCTION: DETAILED ACTION PLAN / Net reduction in CO₂ emissions / -850 kt (-27.5%) / 3 200 / 3 000 / 2 800 / 2 600 / 2 400 / 2 200 / 2 000 / 1 800 / 1 600 / 1 400 / 1 200 / 1 000 / 3,090 kt / 2,240 kt / CO₂ Emissions 2019 / Raw materials / Energy efficiency / Greener energy / Prod increase impact* / CO₂ Emissions 2030 / Scope 1 impact / Scope 2 impact / Prod increase impact / ACTION PLAN IN PLACE TO REACH SBT 27.5% REDUCTION TARGET / *Assumption = +1% volume CAGR 2019-2030 (tons) / Verallia's ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 26

**Layout**

Full-width header bar across the top-left with the section number 2 at the far left, then a large left-aligned title beneath it starting at about 2.6% margin; a centred waterfall chart fills the middle band with a y-axis of labelled values at its left, category labels beneath the bars, a bordered callout box in the lower-middle, a left-aligned italic footnote, a footer line bottom-left, a centred logo at the bottom and the page number 26 in the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Waterfall (bridge) bar chart. Y-axis: value scale from 1 000 to 3 200 with labels every 200 at 1 000, 1 200, 1 400, 1 600, 1 800, 2 000, 2 200, 2 400, 2 600, 2 800, 3 000, 3 200, no axis title (values in kt); faint horizontal gridlines in pale teal at each label. X-axis: six categories. Bar 1 'CO₂ Emissions 2019' solid #00A7A7 bar from 1 000 to 3 090 kt, value label '3,090 kt' in white centred inside the bar. Bar 2 'Raw materials' floating bar about 2 750 to 3 090 kt, #67C8C5. Bar 3 'Energy efficiency' floating stack: about 2 700 to 2 750 kt light #C9EAEB segment on top of about 2 410 to 2 700 kt #67C8C5 segment. Bar 4 'Greener energy' floating stack: about 2 100 to 2 410 kt light #C9EAEB segment on top of about 2 030 to 2 100 kt #67C8C5 segment. Bar 5 'Prod increase impact*' floating bar about 2 030 to 2 240 kt, dark teal #017C7C. Bar 6 'CO₂ Emissions 2030' solid #00A7A7 bar from 1 000 to 2 240 kt, value label '2,240 kt' in white centred inside. Net reduction annotation: a grey #7F7F7F bracket line running from above bar 1 (arrow pointing down at its top) across the top to above bar 6 (arrow pointing down at its top), with label 'Net reduction in CO₂ emissions' above a bordered box reading '-850 kt (-27.5%)'. Legend at lower-middle left of the chart: three swatches stacked, #67C8C5 for 'Scope 1 impact', #C9EAEB for 'Scope 2 impact', #00A7A7 for 'Prod increase impact'. No values are printed for the intermediate bars; their values above are estimates marked about.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with teal palette. Title in Bahnschrift regular uppercase, teal #01A5A5, roughly 5.9% of slide height cap-height area; body labels in Bahnschrift regular about 2.0% of height; axis labels dark grey #595959, category labels grey #7F7F7F, teal category labels #01A5A5 for the two total bars. Accents: teal #00A7A7 bars, light teal #67C8C5, pale #C9EAEB, dark #017C7C. Header bar fill #78CFCF with white uppercase Bahnschrift text. Callout box has white fill and a 1px teal-blue #0E8FB0 outline with teal text. Gridlines pale teal hairlines. Bottom-right corner has a light teal quarter-circle shape at about 25% opacity with the page number in white. No shadows or gradients; square corners on bars and boxes.

### Slide 27

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO₂ EMISSIONS ACROSS OUR OPERATIONS / CO₂ RELATED INVESTMENTS / CASH OUT BY CATEGORY in € million / 28 / 28 / 27 / 24 / 24 / 21 / 19 / 15 / 13 / 12 / 6 / 3 / 2019 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 2025 2026 2027 2028 2029 2030 / Raw mat / Energy efficiency / Greener energy / • Total investment required to reach 2030 CO₂ / reduction target is estimated at ca. €220m, / with some investments still under evaluation / • An estimated €180m (out of €220m) will be / treated as Verallia Strategic Capex / (ie outside of 8% recurring capex envelope) / • Bulk of Capex linked to energy efficiency / as other sources of CO₂ reduction will / involve changes in raw material or energy / purchasing mix / ~€220m BY 2030 / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 27

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout with a left-aligned title block at the top, a stacked bar chart occupying the left half below the subtitle, and a three-bullet text column on the right half, with a teal callout bar at the lower right. A teal section tag bar runs across the top-left corner with the section number to its left. The footer carries the source line at bottom left, the Verallia logo at bottom centre and the slide number at bottom right inside a corner curve. Margins are about 2.7% left; the bullet column starts at about 59% x and the chart's year labels sit on a shared baseline.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical stacked bar chart, title 'CASH OUT BY CATEGORY in € million' (grey subtitle above the plot, left aligned). Y axis: no axis labels or gridlines printed; values are read from segment labels only, total value labelled above each bar. X axis: categories 2019 to 2030 (years, grey #BFBFBF), no unit printed. Series (stack order bottom to top): Raw mat (dark teal #01A5A5), Energy efficiency (mid teal #67C8C5), Greener energy (pale teal #C9EAEB). Printed total labels per year: 2019 = 6; 2020 = 3; 2021 = 15; 2022 = 28; 2023 = 24; 2024 = 28; 2025 = 27; 2026 = 19; 2027 = 12; 2028 = 21; 2029 = 24; 2030 = 13. Segment values not printed; estimates: 2019 Raw mat 0, Energy efficiency about 6, Greener energy 0; 2020 Raw mat about 1, Energy efficiency about 0, Greener energy about 2; 2021 Raw mat about 12, Energy efficiency about 2, Greener energy about 1; 2022 Raw mat about 13, Energy efficiency about 12, Greener energy about 3; 2023 Raw mat about 6, Energy efficiency about 9, Greener energy about 9; 2024 Raw mat about 0.5, Energy efficiency about 23, Greener energy about 4; 2025 Raw mat about 1, Energy efficiency about 25, Greener energy about 1; 2026 Raw mat about 5, Energy efficiency about 8, Greener energy about 3; 2027 Raw mat 0, Energy efficiency about 9, Greener energy about 3; 2028 Raw mat 0, Energy efficiency about 19, Greener energy about 2; 2029 Raw mat 0, Energy efficiency about 22, Greener energy about 2; 2030 Raw mat about 3, Energy efficiency about 7, Greener energy about 3. Legend: horizontal, below the axis, three entries with filled circle markers (Raw mat dark teal, Energy efficiency mid teal, Greener energy pale teal), grey text. No highlighted points. Callout: teal rectangle with a left-pointing chevron notch containing white text '~€220m BY 2030'. Logo: Verallia logo (teal leaf-like mark plus grey-teal wordmark 'verallia') at bottom centre. Footer: no image besides logo; a thin teal bar at bottom left under the source line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 28

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY – SIGNIFICANTLY REDUCE OUR CO2 EMISSIONS ACROSS OUR OPERATIONS / GREEN ELECTRICITY ACTION PLAN AND ACHIEVEMENTS / % Green Electricity / Our target: / 60% / Green / electricity / in 2025* / 70 / 60 / 50 / 40 / 30 / 20 / 10 / 0 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / GREEN ELECTRICITY PURCHASES / ON-SITE ELECTRICITY GENERATION / Policy and / objectives / • Maximize green electricity purchases through certified sources of electricity / (driving zero emission factor), according to availability in each country / • Target: cover at least 60%* of Group electricity consumption with / certified energy by 2025 / • On-site storage/warehouses offer plenty / of surface for solar panels installation / • Exploring potential for solar panel installation / in plants with sufficient sun exposure / Achievements / to date / • 100% of the 3 Brazilian plants energy needs come from hydroelectricity already / • Argentina’s Mendoza plant uses wind-powered energy for 47% of its electricity / needs / • In 2021, Verallia Iberia has secured 100% of its electricity needs from renewable / sources / • A first project is launched in Verallia’s / Mondego plant in Portugal for SOP 2022 / • Targeting 11 equipped plants by 2030 / * Including Low Carbon electricity in France / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / verallia / 28

**Layout**

Two-column body grid under a full-width title, with a chart and target badge in the upper half. A teal header bar sits at the top left with the section number 2 and the CSR strategy line in white caps. The title sits at top left below the bar, left-aligned at about 2.5% margin. The upper band holds the 'Our target:' label and 60% badge at left and the bar chart at right. Two section headings with flanking rules divide the lower area into left and right columns. Vertical teal label boxes at the far left hold 'Policy and objectives' and 'Achievements to date'. The footnote sits at bottom left, the footer text at bottom left, the Verallia logo centred at the bottom, and the page number 28 bottom right on a pale teal corner.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical column chart titled '% Green Electricity' (centred above the chart). Y axis 0 to 70, gridlines every 10, no unit label printed (unit is percent). X axis years 2020 to 2025. Values are not printed on the slide; estimates: 2020 about 33%, 2021 about 44%, 2022 about 45%, 2023 about 50%, 2024 about 50%, 2025 about 61%. Bars 2020 to 2024 are light teal #67C8C5; the 2025 bar is darker teal about #0096B4 and is the highlighted point. No legend. Target badge: a rounded organic blob in teal #00B3B0 with a lighter offset blob behind it at about 28% opacity, containing 60% in large white bold Arial and the words Green electricity in 2025* in white bold Arial. Logo: the Verallia mark, a cluster of overlapping rounded green-teal leaf or drop shapes, beside the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey.

**Design**

Background plain white #FFFFFF. Title in teal #01A5A5, Bahnschrift regular, all caps, about 5.9% of slide height. Header text white Bahnschrift caps on #78CFCF bar, about 2.0%. Body bullets in grey #7F7F7F Bahnschrift regular, about 2.6% of slide height. Section headings in teal #01A5A5 Bahnschrift caps. Sidebar labels in white Bahnschrift regular on #67C8C5. Chart axis labels in #595959 Bahnschrift about 1.7%. Accents #00B3B0, #78CFCF, #67C8C5, #0096B4. Thin teal rules about 1 px. Rectangles are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients. Organic blob badge has rounded irregular corners. Footnote italic grey #808080 about 1.3%. Footer #BFBFBF about 1.5%. Page number white about 2.2% on pale teal corner.

29

**Layout**

A small teal section tab sits at the top left above a large all-caps title left-aligned at about 2.5% from the left edge. Below is a full-width four-column table band, then a teal banner offset to the right of centre; beneath it a left column of four right-aligned icon-and-label rows, a central wine column and a right syrup column each with a product photo and bullets. The footer sits bottom left, the logo bottom centre and the page number bottom right on a teal corner.

**Visuals**

Table: four columns, OUR GOAL (wide), PERFORMANCE INDICATORS (wide), 2019, 2025 TARGETS; one body row. OUR GOAL cell: 'Revert' and 'average weight' and '3%' in teal #01A5A5, rest grey #5A5A5A. Performance cell: 'Alpha index' with formula line beneath. 2019 value 16 (grey). 2025 TARGETS value 15.5 (teal) on a pale #C9EAEB cell at 90% opacity. Header cells teal #78CFCF with white caps; thin white vertical dividers between columns; teal rule under the table. Banner: teal #78CFCF rectangle with a small white triangular notch at its left end. Four line-style icons in teal #01A5A5 at left: (1) a filled circle, a rotated square and a triangle arranged as a shape icon, beside OPTIMIZE WEIGHT AND SHAPE; (2) a pallet with three cardboard boxes stacked on it, beside OPTIMIZE PALLETIZATION; (3) two circular arrows chasing each other, beside AVOID RECYCLING TROUBLEMAKERS; (4) a globe with a location pin, beside FAVOR LOCAL NETWORKS. Photo 1: a red wine bottle standing upright, centred, full view with no crop, dark purple capsule and neck, dark burgundy glass, dark purple label with gold MANSO and de VELASCO lettering, a gold emblem, OLD VINES text, Cabernet Sauvignon line and MIGUEL TORRES and Chile marks at the foot; soft even studio lighting, white background. Behind it a pale teal blob shape at 25% opacity. Photo 2: an amber-brown syrup bottle standing upright, black cap, white neck label reading VEDRENNE, a blackcurrant-coloured front label with a small hot-air balloon logo, a photo of black currants and leaves, the word VEDRENNE, CASSIS BLACKCURRANT text; lighting soft and even, with a pale teal blob shape at 25% opacity behind its neck. Verallia logo: a teal and green leaf-like mark beside a dark grey lowercase wordmark verallia.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Titles and section headings in teal #01A5A5 Bahnschrift regular uppercase, sans-serif. Body and table text in grey #5A5A5A and #7F7F7F with teal #01A5A5 and #00A7A7 accents and highlights. Footer text #BFBFBF. Section tab and table headers #78CFCF with white text. Title is about 5.9% of slide height (cap height about 4%); body text about 2.2% of slide height; table text 2.6%. Flat shapes, no shadows, no gradients, square corners on the table and banner; the teal blobs are organic rounded shapes at 25% opacity.

30

**Layout**

Two-part layout on a white page: a full-width table band and a centred section divider run across the upper half, while the lower half splits into a left column of three text-and-icon action blocks and a right column of three organic blob-shaped photos with the VCS logo. The title sits top-left at x 2.5% below a teal section band that starts at the top edge (left margin about 2.7%). The footnote is left-aligned at the bottom of the left column, the footer text sits bottom-left, the Verallia logo is centred at the bottom, and the page number 30 sits bottom-right on a teal corner. The section number 2 sits left of the header band.

**Visuals**

Icon 1: solid teal #01A5A5 silhouette of a tree with a round, lobed crown on a short trunk, at about x 3.0% y 44.0% w 4.0% h 7.0%. Icon 2: solid teal #01A5A5 cloud shape with the white text CO2 (subscript 2) centred inside, at about x 3.0% y 59.7% w 3.8% h 5.0%. Icon 3: solid teal #01A5A5 factory silhouette with two tall chimneys on the left and a row of small white window squares along the base, at about x 2.8% y 72.9% w 4.0% h 6.2%. Photo A (forest, x 74.5% y 40.1% w 11.6% h 20.0%, cropped into an organic blob shape with rounded lobes, sitting over a light teal tint): a tall tree trunk rises through dense green tropical foliage, a person in light clothing stands at the right by the trunk; natural daylight, pale sky visible at top; dominant greens and white-blue sky; no overlay. Photo B (portrait, x 86.3% y 56.0% w 11.6% h 20.0%, blob-cropped, over a teal tint): a smiling middle-aged man in a green t-shirt holds a small seedling in a plastic pot with both hands, blurred green trees behind; soft daylight; dominant greens with warm skin tones; no overlay. Photo C (group, x 75.1% y 72.5% w 11.6% h 20.0%, blob-cropped): several adults and children gather on bare orange-brown soil among young trees, some planting and some standing; warm sunlight; dominant ochre, brown and green; no overlay. VCS logo (x 88.7% y 44.8% w 7.4% h 7.3%): a stylised blue-navy VCS letter mark with a green-teal check-like accent, followed by the words VERIFIED CARBON STANDARD in dark grey stacked caps and A Global Benchmark for Carbon in small teal italic beneath. Verallia logo (x 46.1% y 94.6% w 7.8% h 4.3%): a teal-green rounded emblem of overlapping circles beside the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey. Table (x 2.7% y 19.3% w 94.9% h 12.8%): 3 columns and 3 rows. Header row: OUR GOAL | PERFORMANCE INDICATORS | 2019. Row 1: Plant 100,000 trees per year | Trees planted/year | 100,000. Row 2: Offset all professional travels emissions every year | Purchase Certified Carbon Credits | New objective*. No chart values.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white slide with teal accents. Titles in teal #01A5A5 Bahnschrift regular uppercase (DIN-style condensed grotesque sans-serif); the main title is about 5.9% of slide height, the table and section label about 2.6%, body text about 2.0% to 2.2%, footnote about 1.3%, footer about 1.5%. Body grey #7F7F7F for descriptions, #595959 for table text, #808080 for footnote (italic), #BFBFBF for footer. Accent teal #01A5A5 for headings, sublines and the 100,000 trees and Offset all professional travels highlights; header band fill #78CFCF with white uppercase text; table header cells fill #78CFCF; section label fill #F6F6F6 with a thin grey rule behind it; table body white with thin light-teal #78CFCF dividers and a thick #01A5A5 bottom rule. Shapes are rectangular with square corners except the organic blob photo masks and corner tint. Teal tint #01A5A5 at 25% opacity used for the blob shadows behind photos and the bottom-right corner shape. No gradients or shadows.

### Slide 32

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The safety slide places the 2019 and 2025 targets beside the frequency measure in a compact layout that makes the gap to target clear.
- Worth keeping: Keep the agenda tracker with presenter photos, the goal-and-indicator table header used on slides 14, 22, 29, 32 and 35, the waterfall on slide 26 with its single takeaway box, and the lever 1/2/3 structure on slides 23 to 25.

**Text**

2
CSR STRATEGY - PROVIDE A SAFE & INCLUSIVE PLACE OF WORK
PROVIDE A SAFE PLACE OF WORK
SAFETY: FREQUENCY OF ACCIDENTS
OUR GOALS
PERFORMANCE INDICATORS
2019
2025 TARGETS
Aim for “zero accident” every year
TF2 (= all accidents / million hours worked)*
5.5
< 2
*Includes temporary employees
Respect for people, one of Verallia’s values => first priority ensuring our employees and contractors a safe environment
Standards completed by Safety rules designed to help employees recognize and avoid risky situations, Verallia cardinal rules
Organization every year of Verallia Safety Day: opportunity to engage every employee & manager on the journey to 0 accident
17 EHS standards developed & covering all aspects of safety in its plants. Strict application is controlled via on-site audits
Roadmap developed to support 0 accident target
Roadmap now being deployed in each site
2020: TF2 reduced by 17%, in line with target to reach TF2 < 2 by 2025
32
Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Two-column text layout on a white slide: a rounded photograph sits at the left, a bold-free table spans the top right, and six text blocks sit in two columns on the right (left column three blocks, right column three blocks) with thin teal vertical rules before each block. The title block sits top-left under a teal header tab, with the slide number 2 at the top-left corner; the footer text sits bottom-left, the Verallia logo is centred at the bottom, and the page number 32 sits bottom-right on a teal corner shape. Left margin about 2.5% and right margin about 2.5% of slide width.

**Visuals**

One photograph and one table, plus a small logo. Photograph: two male factory workers in grey caps, safety glasses, red ear defenders and high-visibility vests, handling a glass bottle on a metal fixture with a threaded rod; one worker in the foreground wears grey work gloves and the man in front holds the bottle. Shallow depth of field with blurred machinery in the background; cool grey, teal and olive-green tones. The photo is cropped into an irregular organic rounded blob shape with no border and overlaps a pale teal shape at its lower-left edge. Table: 4 columns (OUR GOALS | PERFORMANCE INDICATORS | 2019 | 2025 TARGETS) with a teal header row and one body row: Aim for “zero accident” every year | TF2 (= all accidents / million hours worked)* | 5.5 | < 2. No chart values. Logo: Verallia logo at bottom centre, a green-teal leaf/droplet mark with the lowercase word verallia.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with teal accents and grey body text. Title in Bahnschrift-style condensed sans-serif capitals, teal #00A4A3 with the words SAFE PLACE in #01A5A5, about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in the same family, grey #7F7F7F, about 4.4% of slide height. Table header cells filled #78CFCF with white capital labels about 2.2% of height; body row text #5A5A5A with teal #01A5A5 accent for zero accident and < 2; a teal #01A5A5 rule under the table. Body text in Bahnschrift regular, grey #7F7F7F, about 2.8% of slide height, with bold 2020 at the start of the final block. Thin teal vertical rules #01A5A5 at each block. Footnote in italic Calibri, #808080, about 1.7%. Footer text in light grey #BFBFBF about 1.5%. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; the decorative blobs are teal at 25% opacity.

33

**Layout**

Two-part slide on a white field: a teal section tag runs across the top left above a large all-caps title with a grey subtitle beneath it, with the title flush to a left margin of about 2.5 percent of slide width. The body is in three bands: a four-row level list on the left with a vertical divider and a headline statement on the right, then a row of three panels (LATAM stat text, LATAM bar chart, multi-country bar chart) above a full-width teal banner, with a footer line at bottom left, the Verallia logo at bottom centre and the page number 33 at bottom right on a corner shape.

**Visuals**

Chart 1: vertical bar chart titled LATAM TF2* with a single series of teal bars (#01A5A5) for 2018 (about 2.9), 2019 (about 1.2) and 2020 (about 0.85); y-axis 0 to 3.5 in steps of 0.5 with decimal commas, no unit printed; a thin horizontal gridline at 1; x-axis labels 2018, 2019, 2020; no legend; no highlighted points. Chart 2: clustered vertical bar chart with no chart title, y-axis 0 to 16 in steps of 4 with gridlines at 4, 8, 12 and 16, no unit printed; two series: light teal (#78CFCF) TF2 - END OF DEC 2019 and dark teal (#01A5A5) TF2 - END OF DEC 2020; categories and approximate values: Verallia Group about 5.5 and about 4.5; France about 16 and about 13; Italy about 2.2 and about 1.8; Iberia about 2.1 and about 2.1; North Europe about 3.4 and about 3.6; Latam about 1.2 and about 0.4; legend at top right with two square swatches. Diagram: four horizontal level lines, each ending in a teal dot on the right, with the level labels right-aligned to the left of each line; a thin teal chevron-shaped arrow pointing right at the divider. Banner: a teal rectangle with a notched left end (chevron) carrying the call-to-action text. Logo: Verallia logo at bottom centre. Corner shape: a translucent teal quarter-round shape at bottom right behind the page number.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with teal and grey type in Bahnschrift (a DIN-style sans-serif, regular and semibold weights). Title in all-caps teal #01A5A5 at about 5.9 percent of slide height; subtitle CASE STUDY in all-caps grey #7F7F7F at about 4.4 percent. Section tag: light teal #78CFCF box with white all-caps text about 2.0 percent of height. Level labels grey #7F7F7F (about 2.6 percent) and level names teal #01A5A5 (about 3.0 percent). Body descriptions grey #7F7F7F at about 2.0 percent. Headline statement in teal #01A5A5 at about 3.7 percent; the lower statement in dark grey #5A5A5A at about 3.7 percent. Chart text dark grey #595959 at about 1.5 to 1.7 percent. Footnote italic grey #7F7F7F at about 1.3 percent. Footer #BFBFBF at about 1.5 percent. Accents: light teal #78CFCF and teal #01A5A5. Lines are thin, about 0.1 to 0.2 percent of height, teal; dots are solid teal; bars are flat with no shadows or gradients and square corners; banner has square corners.

### Slide 34

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY - PROVIDE A SAFE & INCLUSIVE PLACE OF WORK / PROVIDE AN INCLUSIVE PLACE TO WORK / GEOGRAPHICAL DIVERSITY / 10,000 employees from 63 nationalities / working for the Group / Located across 11 manufacturing / countries / 13% / 25% / Geographical / 17% / diversity / France / 14% / Italy / 17% / Iberia / 14% / Germany / Eastern Europe / 34 / Latam / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Three-column composition on a white field: a stacked column of three black-and-white photographs at the left edge, a centred content column with the section heading, two statements and a donut chart, and a light-grey world map panel at the right edge. Both the photo stack and the map panel have concave curved inner edges with a thin teal arc beside them. The slide title sits top-left at about 4.4% from the top, starting at about 2.5% from the left, above the content. A teal section tab runs across the top-left corner. The footer carries a source line at bottom-left, the verallia logo centred at the bottom, and the slide number 34 in white on a light teal corner shape at bottom-right. There is no subtitle beneath the title and the upper middle of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

Three stacked black-and-white photographs at the left edge, each cropped as a horizontal strip with white gaps between them. Top strip (about 27% wide): factory interior with rows of machinery and a worker in a cap at the right edge, looking left, grey tones. Middle strip (about 27% wide): close-up of a smiling male worker in safety glasses and a hard hat, lit from the front, dark background. Bottom strip (about 28% wide): two workers in white hard hats and hi-vis vests in an industrial plant with pipework behind, greyscale. The strips are full-bleed to the left edge and their right edges are cut by the curved white arc. Centre: a donut chart titled Geographical diversity in the centre hole, six segments starting at 12 o'clock and running clockwise: France 25% (#0098AF), Italy 14% (#009362), Iberia 14% (#F9B200), Germany 17% (#EA640D), Eastern Europe 17% (#E32334), Latam 13% (#DC0064). Segment labels are printed in white inside each segment. Legend to the right of the donut lists the six series top to bottom with matching colour squares. Values sum to 100%. No axes, no highlighted points. Right: a light-grey (#F2F2F2 to #E6E6E6) flat world map in Robinson-like projection with grey country names in small sans-serif type, and about 11 teal (#009AA5) map pins: one over Russia at the upper right, about 7 clustered over Northern Europe (United Kingdom, France, Germany, Poland, Italy area) and Ukraine, one over Brazil, and two over Argentina area. Visible map labels include Groenland, Islande, Finlande, Suède, Norvège, Royaume-Uni, Pologne, Ukraine, France, Italie, Russie, Canada, États-Unis, Mexique, Venezuela, Colombie, Pérou, Bolivie, Brésil, Argentine, Chili, Algérie, Libye, Égypte, Mali, Niger, Tchad, Nigeria, Soudan, Éthiopie, RD Congo, Kenya, Tanzanie, Angola, Namibie, Botswana, Afrique du Sud, Madagascar, Arabie saoudite, Turquie, Irak, Iran, Kazakhstan, Afghanistan, Pakistan, Inde, Thaïlande, Mongolie, Chine, Corée du Sud, Japon, Indonésie, Papouasie, Australie, and ocean labels Océan Atlantique Nord, Océan Atlantique Sud, Océan Pacifique Sud, Océan Indien, Océan Austral. The map is full-bleed to the right edge and cut at the top and bottom of the content band.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a teal and grey palette. Title is set in Bahnschrift (DIN-style condensed grotesque sans-serif), regular weight, all capitals, colour teal #01A5A5, about 5.9% of slide height. Section heading GEOGRAPHICAL DIVERSITY is Bahnschrift regular capitals in teal #01A5A5, about 3.7% of slide height. Statements are Bahnschrift medium/regular in grey #7F7F7F, about 2.6% of slide height, centred. Chart labels are Bahnschrift in #595959 at about 1.7% of slide height; percentage labels are white Bahnschrift at about 2.0%. Section tab is light teal #78CFCF with white Bahnschrift capitals at about 2.0%, the number 2 in teal at about 3.3%. Footer text is Bahnschrift light in #BFBFBF at about 1.5%. Page number 34 is white Bahnschrift at about 2.2%. Corner shape and arc accents are teal #78CFCE and light teal #01A5A5 at 25% opacity. Shapes have no shadows, no rounded corners and no gradients; photos are hard-edged rectangles.

GENDER EQUALITY
• In 2019, 29% of female managers at Group level
• Proactive action plan (recruitment, compensation, career development “Women at Verallia”, mentoring by Comex, no tolerance for sexism and discrimination)
• Increasing of the % of women in all management committees to 30%
• Gender equality index based on 5 indicators: individual compensation, beneficiaries of individual increase, beneficiaries of promotions, % of employees increased upon return from maternity leave, number of employees of the under-represented gender among the 10 highest compensations of the company
HIRING OF DISABLED STAFF

Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021
35

**Layout**

Two-column body below a full-width title block, with the title at top left and the subtitle OBJECTIVES directly beneath it in grey. A four-column goals table spans the full width beneath a teal rule, followed by two columns (gender equality on the left, disabled hiring on the right) with icon blobs and headings, and a logo cluster at bottom right. The Verallia logo sits centred in the footer, the footer text sits bottom left, and the page number 35 sits bottom right on a pale teal corner shape; margins are about 2.7% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Table: four columns (OUR GOALS, PERFORMANCE INDICATORS, 2019, 2025 TARGETS) and two body rows. Row 1: Increase gender equality index in all countries by 15 points | Gender equality index (as defined by French law) | 60 | 75. Row 2: Favor insertion by doubling the ratio of disabled employees from 3% in 2019 to 6% in 2025 | % of disabled people (according to national definitions) | 3% | 6%. Header cells are teal, the 2025 TARGETS value cells are pale mint. Icon 1: white male and female pictogram on a teal organic blob. Icon 2: white wheelchair-user pictogram on a teal organic blob. Photograph: a wheelchair rugby player in a white jersey with number 9, holding a ball, seen from the side at close range with a blurred dark background and red and blue wheelchair parts, cropped into an organic blob shape, with a teal blob behind it at lower opacity. Logo: Ministère des Armées emblem (blue and red mark) with the wordmark and the motto Liberté, Égalité, Fraternité, placed inside a thin teal outlined blob. Verallia logo: a teal-green leaf-like mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia, centred in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and accents in teal #01A5A5, subtitle and most body text in grey #7F7F7F, goal and indicator text in dark grey #5A5A5A, table headers white text on #78CFCF, pale table tint #E8F7F6, footer text #BFBFBF. Typefaces: Bahnschrift, a DIN-style sans-serif, in regular weight, with title in capitals at about 5.9% of slide height, body at about 2.6% and footer at about 1.5%. Shapes are organic blobs with no shadows or gradients; the table has thin light-teal row dividers and a thick teal rule beneath it.

### Slide 36

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY - PROVIDE A SAFE & INCLUSIVE PLACE OF WORK / PROVIDE AN INCLUSIVE PLACE TO WORK / ENCOURAGE EMPLOYEE SHAREHOLDING OWNERSHIP / 8 / countries / verallia / 2020 / Shareholding / Offer / 42% / participation rate* / a subscription rate / of close to / 80% in France / 50% in Spain / 30% in Italy & Brazil / 3.3% / of share capital held / by employees / PERFORMANCE INDICATORS / 2019 / 2020 / 2025 TARGETS / % of Verallia share capital held by / employees (directly or through / FCPE) / 2.6% / 3.3% / 5% / In June 2020, / close to 3,300 employees took part / in the Employee Shareholding Offer / Around 37% of our employees / all over the world are shareholders / of the company / ANNUAL EMPLOYEE OFFERS WILL BE RENEWED IN 2021 AND IN THE COMING YEARS / * Amongst eligible employees worldwide / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 36

**Layout**

Full-bleed white slide with a left-aligned title block at top left (section tag bar above it, then a two-line title, the second line grey) and a left column of timeline graphics with the Verallia 2020 logo block. The right half holds a three-column performance table at top and two centred statement blocks below it; a full-width teal banner sits near the bottom, with a right-aligned footnote beneath it, a footer line at bottom left, a centred logo at bottom and the page number at bottom right inside a corner shape.

**Visuals**

Vertical curved timeline on the left, read top to bottom: a thin teal arc enters from the left edge and runs to a ring marker, then a location-pin icon, a ring marker with the label 42%, a handshake icon, a ring marker with the subscription-rate label, a banknote icon, a ring marker with the 3.3% label, and a group-of-people icon at the bottom. Ring markers are teal outline circles with a solid teal centre dot, joined by thin teal connector lines. Icons are teal line drawings on white. Large Verallia logo at left: a stylised teal-green glossy droplet cluster in gradient teal to green, with the dark grey wordmark verallia to its right. Table at top right: four columns (PERFORMANCE INDICATORS, 2019, 2020, 2025 TARGETS) and one body row with values 2.6%, 3.3% and 5% (the 5% cell is teal on a pale mint cell); a solid teal rule underlines the table. Short thin teal horizontal divider between the two statement blocks. Teal banner with a notched arrow outline at its left end. Small Verallia logo centred in the footer. No photographs.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Titles teal #01A5A5 in a DIN-style sans-serif (Bahnschrift), regular weight, all capitals; subtitle and section tag text grey #7F7F7F with the section tag in white on a light teal bar #78CFCF. Body statements dark grey #5A5A5A, with key figures and phrases in teal #01A5A5. Title cap height is about 5.9% of slide height; statement text about 3.7%; labels and footnotes 1.5 to 2.6%. Table header fill #78CFCF with white capitals; body cells white with pale mint #E8F7F6 for the 2025 column; teal rule #01A5A5 under the table. Banner fill #78CFCF with white capitals. Flat design: no shadows, gradients or rounded corners; corner accent is a quarter-circle teal at 25% opacity.

### Slide 37

**Text**

2 / CSR STRATEGY - PROVIDE A SAFE & INCLUSIVE PLACE OF WORK / OUR SUPPORTIVE COMMITMENT TO THE COMMUNITY / GROUP INITIATIVES / COUNTRIES INITIATIVES / Partnership with / AP-HP, to support Covidom / an application for home monitoring / of COVID-19 patients with the help / of 2,500 volunteers / Partnership with / Le Secours Populaire Français / (to act against exclusion) / Partnership with / La Maison des femmes / (Women’s House), a medical-social structure / dedicated to women in difficulty / or victims of violence / Customer: financial support, / seeds / Equipment: medical, IT / Food, training, materials / for schools / Transportation support / for medical teams / CEO gave up 2020 variable compensation (50% of annual compensation) / Executive Committee members gave up 15% of annual compensation / Total €1.6m to finance donations / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 37

**Layout**

Single-slide template with the section tag in the top-left corner and a large title directly beneath it, left-aligned at about 2.5% from the left edge. A full-width teal horizontal rule at about 21% of the height divides the title from the content; a second rule at about 62% separates the content from the bottom statement. Content is in four columns: three under a centred GROUP INITIATIVES heading (columns centred near x 15%, 43% and 66%) and one left-aligned column under COUNTRIES INITIATIVES at x 80%. The three CEO and committee statements are centred on the slide below the lower rule. Footer text sits bottom-left at about 96.7% height, the Verallia logo is bottom-centre, and the slide number 37 sits white on a pale teal corner shape at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Five images and one logo. (1) Laptop and smartphone mockup at x 6.1%, y 41.9%, 12.2% by 13.5%: a dark-framed laptop with a white screen showing the Covidom home-monitoring web application (pale blue header area, list panels and blue buttons) and a black smartphone beside it showing a small app screen; both devices are shown on a white and light grey backdrop, full object visible, not cropped, boxed by a faint light-grey rectangle. (2) Secours Populaire Français logo at x 31.8%, y 40.5%, 8.8% by 14.4%: a red, white and blue dove-like hand graphic in the French tricolour colours (red, blue and white strokes) with the hand-lettered words SECOURS POPULAIRE FRANÇAIS in blue, red and grey script; a pale teal blob sits behind its lower right, and the logo is set on a white rectangle. (3) Photograph at x 59.1%, y 41.6%, 8.9% by 13.4%: exterior of La Maison des femmes building, a timber-slatted pitched-roof structure at left with a glass and brightly coloured (pink, magenta and blue) facade at right, seen in daylight with greenery at left edge; the photo is cropped into an irregular rounded blob shape with a pale teal blob overlapping its top right. (4) Small inset at x 64.4%, y 42.4%, 3.0% by 4.7%: a red and pink sign reading LA MAISON DES FEMMES on the building facade, with a small blue-grey sun icon above it, overlaid on the teal blob. (5) Verallia logo at x 46.1%, y 94.6%, 7.8% by 4.3%: a teal-green stylised double-drop emblem followed by the dark grey lowercase wordmark verallia.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the whole slide. Clean white corporate slide with teal accents. Section tag is a pale teal bar with white uppercase sans text; the title is large teal uppercase Bahnschrift at about 5.9% of slide height for capitals. Column headings and bottom statements are teal; column lead text and body copy are mid grey. Body text is about 2.2% to 2.6% of slide height, the bottom statements about 4.4% to 5.2%. Thin teal hairline rules (about 1 pt) run across the slide. Partner names are set in teal, lead-in phrases in grey. Corners are square; a pale teal translucent blob (about 28% opacity) and a large translucent teal corner shape at bottom-right (25% opacity) are the only decorative shapes. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 38

**Text**

2
CSR STRATEGY - PROVIDE A SAFE & INCLUSIVE PLACE OF WORK
OUR SUPPORTIVE COMMITMENT TO THE COMMUNITY
FROM 2021, €1.5m, EVERY YEAR
dedicated to local projects in line with our Purpose,
targeting circular economy and inclusion
Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021
38

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a teal section tag and a large teal all-caps title in the top-left margin, starting at about 2.5% from the left edge. A full-bleed photograph band runs edge to edge from about 24.8% to 88.5% of the slide height, with a dark translucent overlay carrying the centred white statement. The footer sits on a single baseline near the bottom: grey footer text at left, the Verallia logo centred, and the page number on a pale teal corner arc at bottom right.

**Visuals**

One photograph and one logo. The photograph is an overhead close-up of several people's hands stacked together in a central huddle, with grey, white and light blue sleeves at the edges of the frame. Skin tones are pale to tan, with grey-blue and dark grey surroundings. The image is full-bleed across the slide width and cropped at top and bottom, with a black tint at 44% opacity covering its central band from 35.0% to 75.2% of slide height. The logo is the Verallia mark: a green-teal overlapping swirl symbol followed by the lowercase grey wordmark 'verallia'. Decorative: a pale teal corner arc at bottom right and a teal bar at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a full-bleed photograph band across the middle of the slide. Clean white corporate look. Background is white #FFFFFF. Section tag is a #78CFCF rectangle with white capitals; the slide number '2' beside it is #78CFCF. Main title is #01A5A5 teal, all capitals, regular weight, about 5.9% of slide height. Overlay statement is white #FFFFFF, regular weight, with '€1.5m' larger at about 14.8% and 'FROM 2021' and 'EVERY YEAR' about 7.4%, and the two sub-lines about 5.4%. Footer text is #BFBFBF at about 1.5%. Typeface is Bahnschrift, a DIN-style condensed-leaning sans-serif. No gradients, shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 39

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear route: an agenda that is repeated as a tracker (slides 2, 3, 10, 39, 48), consistent section tags, and three pillars each opened by a photo divider and followed by goal tables with 2019 baselines and 2025 targets.

**Text**

GENDA / INTRODUCTION / CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS / GOVERNANCE / CONCLUSION / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Laetitia FABRE / Head of CSR / Romain BARRAL / Director of Operations / Denis MICHEL / Secretary General / Wendy KOOL-FOULON / General Counsel / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Verallia's ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 39

**Layout**

Four equal columns of section tiles spread across the slide below a top-left title, with a centred row of names beneath each tile. The title GENDA sits at top left (x 9.9%, y 5.8%). Each column holds a rounded blob image, a short horizontal rule, an uppercase section label and one or more portrait photos with name and role captions. Footer line sits bottom left, the verallia logo bottom centre and the page number bottom right in a corner tab. Margins are about 3% left and right.

**Visuals**

Four rounded blob-framed images, one per agenda section, left to right: INTRODUCTION (a photograph of glass bottles and jars, greyed out), CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS (a split photograph: left a clear glass bottle, right a close-up of leaves and a white infinity-like line drawn across the join), GOVERNANCE (an illustration of six stylised hands clasped in a ring, in orange, yellow, olive, teal, slate and gold, inside a white blob with a teal blob behind it, the only fully coloured item), and CONCLUSION (a photograph of many glass bottles and jars, greyed out). Portraits: Michel GIANNUZZI (man smiling, head and shoulders, greyed, under INTRODUCTION), Laetitia FABRE (heavily pixelated low-resolution woman portrait, greyed, under CSR), Romain BARRAL (man in suit, greyed, under CSR), Denis MICHEL (man in suit, greyed, under CSR), Wendy KOOL-FOULON (woman with blonde hair smiling, in colour, inside a teal blob, the only active person), Michel GIANNUZZI (man smiling, greyed, under CONCLUSION). Logo: verallia wordmark with a green multi-lobed leaf mark, centred bottom. Overlays: a translucent white veil over the three inactive columns, so only GOVERNANCE is at full strength. Four short horizontal rules (grey, one teal under GOVERNANCE) sit above each label. Photographs are soft, evenly lit, greyscale, cropped into blob shapes and not full-bleed.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title GENDA in teal #01A5A5, regular-weight Bahnschrift-style sans-serif, all capitals, wide letter spacing, about 10% of slide height. Section labels in uppercase Bahnschrift regular about 3% of slide height, grey #7F7F7F for the active label GOVERNANCE and for inactive labels, with inactive columns faded. Names in Bahnschrift regular about 1.7% of slide height in grey #7F7F7F, the active name Wendy KOOL-FOULON in teal #01A5A5 with role General Counsel in teal, about 1.5%. Footer text grey #BFBFBF about 1.5%. Page number white on a light teal tab, about 2.2%. Accents teal #01A5A5 and light teal #78CFCF. Blob shapes have soft organic edges with light grey fills #BFBFBF and #E9EAED and translucent white overlays; no hard shadows. Rules are 4.7% wide, about 0.5% tall.

40

**Layout**

Five columns of content sit under a left-aligned two-line header (large teal title BOARD OF DIRECTORS - PROFILE, grey subtitle line beneath it) with a teal subheading. Columns are NATIONALITY, INDEPENDENCE and DIVERSITY (donut charts with legends and captions), BACKGROUND/SKILLS (horizontal bar chart) and BOARD TRAINING (icon, text and a photo); each column heading is centred over its column with a short teal rule beneath. A full-width banner row at about 87% height carries a book icon and the attendance line between two thin teal rules; the footnote sits right-aligned under it, the footer text bottom-left, the Verallia logo bottom-centre, and the page number 40 bottom-right on a teal corner shape. A section tag GOVERNANCE with number 3 sits in the top-left corner.

**Visuals**

Three donut charts, each with an inner hole and two segments drawn from 12 o'clock: the 60% segment on the left and the 40% segment on the right. Nationality donut: Foreigner 60% in red #EE333E, French 40% in salmon #F58B7A; legend Foreigner (red) and French (salmon). Independence donut: Non independent 60% in magenta #AB1D88, Independent 40% in light magenta #C57CB0; legend Non independent and Independent. Diversity donut: Men 60% in blue #009AD9, Women 40% in light blue #74B9E6; legend Men and Women. Labels 60% and 40% in white are printed on each donut. Bar chart titled BACKGROUND/SKILLS with nine horizontal teal #01A5A5 bars along a thin grey vertical axis, categories listed to the left in dark grey and values printed to the right of each bar: Strategy/Operations 25%, Industry expert 33%, Finance 42%, Entrepreneurship 25%, Human Resources 8%, CSR 25%, Governance/Legal/Regulatory 25%, Marketing/Sales/Communication 17%, Innovation/Digital 8%; no axis numbers or units are printed. Behind the bar labels faint light-grey fragments of the same labels are printed as ghost text. Icons: a teal rounded blob with a white brain-and-gear icon above the bar chart; a teal blob with a white presenter icon above Board Training; a teal blob with a white globe icon beside the nationality caption; a teal blob with a white person-at-desk icon beside the independence caption; a teal blob with a white man-and-woman icon beside the diversity caption; a teal blob with a white open-book icon in the banner. Five round flags in a row beneath the nationality caption: France, United States, Switzerland, Spain and Brazil. Photograph: aerial view of an industrial plant with a grey-roofed factory complex, chimney and surrounding green fields and blue sky, filling a blob-shaped crop with a pale teal blob behind it offset to the right and a white blob behind that; daylight, slightly soft. Logo: verallia wordmark with a green-teal leaf-like mark at bottom centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

Top-left section number 3 and a small teal GOVERNANCE tag sit above a large left-aligned all-caps title spanning the top band; the body is three stacked horizontal rows (Audit, Nomination and Compensation, Sustainable Development), each with a heading, a row of person icons with names, a calendar icon with meeting count, and a topics list in a right column headed TOPICS COVERED, separated by thin vertical teal rules. Legend sits bottom-left, Verallia logo bottom-centre, footer text bottom-left, page number 41 bottom-right on a corner blob. Left margin about 5 percent, right margin about 5 percent.

**Visuals**

Three rows of person icons (12 total) and three calendar icons, all drawn as rounded teardrop-shaped blobs with white silhouettes. Audit row: teal #78CFCF blob with white woman silhouette bearing a white letter C (Marie-José DONSION); grey #A6A6A6 blob with white woman silhouette (Marcia FREITAS); teal #78CFCF blob with white man silhouette (José AROZAMENA). Nomination and Compensation row: teal #78CFCF blob with white woman silhouette bearing a white letter C (Cécile TANDEAU DE MARSAC); grey #A6A6A6 blob with white man silhouette (João SALLES); grey #A6A6A6 blob with white man silhouette (Pierre VAREILLE); teal #78CFCF blob with white woman silhouette (José AROZAMENA). Sustainable Development row: teal #78CFCF blob with white woman silhouette bearing a white letter C (Virginie HELIAS); grey #A6A6A6 blob with white man silhouette (Michel GIANNUZZI); grey #A6A6A6 blob with white man silhouette (Sébastien MOYNOT); grey #A6A6A6 blob with white man silhouette (Dieter MÜLLER); grey #A6A6A6 blob with white man silhouette (Sylvain ARTIGAU). Each row has a dark teal #01A5A5 blob with a white calendar grid icon, with the label 5 plenary meetings in 2020 beneath. A thin teal bracket line under Dieter MÜLLER and Sylvain ARTIGAU labelled Employee-representatives. Small legend icon: light teal #78CFCF blob followed by the italic label Independent member. Verallia logo: colourful overlapping drop shapes in teal and green with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey-teal. Bottom-right corner: a pale teal quarter-circle blob at 25 percent opacity bleeding off the right and bottom edges. Small teal rectangle tag behind GOVERNANCE. Short teal underline bars beneath AUDIT, NOMINATION AND COMPENSATION, SUSTAINABLE DEVELOPMENT and TOPICS COVERED. Thin vertical teal rules at about x 68.8 separating each row from its topics list.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Titles are all-caps Bahnschrift regular in teal #01A5A5, about 5.9 percent of slide height for the main title and about 3.7 percent for section headings. Body and topic text is Bahnschrift regular in teal #01A5A5 at about 2.6 percent of slide height. Member names and meeting labels are Bahnschrift regular in grey #7F7F7F at about 2.2 percent. Legend and note are Bahnschrift italic at about 2.0 percent, in #78CFCF and #808080 respectively, Employee-representatives in #7F7F7F italic. Footer in Bahnschrift regular #BFBFBF at about 1.5 percent. Slide number 41 in white #FFFFFF on the corner blob. Section number 3 in #78CFCF. Shapes: flat teardrop blobs with no shadows or gradients; calendar and icon glyphs in white; thin teal rules about 0.1 percent wide; underline bars about 0.4 percent of height, in #01A5A5.

### Slide 42

**Text**

3 / GOVERNANCE / OUR ESG ACCOUNTABLE GOVERNANCE / TOP / MANAGEMENT / Board of / Directors / Approves the sustainability strategy and monitors its progress and performance / Sustainable / Development / Committee / Ensures that issues relating to social and environmental responsibility are considered in the Group's strategy / and its implementation / Reviews the Group's commitments to sustainability in light of the challenges specific to its business and objectives / Executive / Committee / Defines and implements the Group's sustainability strategy / Sustainability / department / and its network / The Group's Sustainability officer works on a daily basis in coordination with a network of 8 Sustainability correspondents / in each country (e.g. Germany, France, Italy) or region (e.g. Iberia covering Spain and Portugal) / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 42

**Layout**

Single-column governance chart on a white field. The title sits top-left at about x 2.5% and y 4.4%, under a small section tag GOVERNANCE at top-left. Four teal label boxes stack down the left at x 4%, each with descriptive text aligned to its right; two thin grey horizontal rules divide the top block from the lower rows, and a teal blob labelled TOP MANAGEMENT sits top-right overlapping the upper rule. Footer carries the deck title at bottom-left, the Verallia logo centred at the bottom, and the page number 42 at bottom-right on a corner shape.

**Visuals**

Diagram: four teal rectangular boxes stacked vertically on the left, labelled Board of Directors (top, about 4.0% x 22.9% y, 11.8% wide, 10.5% high), Sustainable Development Committee (tall box, 4.0% x 35.1% y, 11.8% wide, 23.5% high), Executive Committee (4.0% x 60.3% y, 11.8% wide, 10.5% high), and Sustainability department and its network (4.0% x 76.2% y, 11.8% wide, 14.9% high). Each box is filled #01A5A5 with white centred text and square corners, and no arrows connect them. A teal organic blob shape labelled TOP MANAGEMENT sits at top-right (83.8% x, 12.1% y, 10.8% wide, 16.6% high) with white text. Two thin grey rules (#595959, about 0.1% stroke) run horizontally across the slide: one at about 20% y above the first row and one at about 73.3% y between the Executive and Sustainability rows. A Verallia logo (teal-green swirl mark with wordmark) sits at bottom centre at 46.1% x, 94.6% y, 7.8% wide, 4.3% high. Bottom-right corner has a pale teal quarter-circle shape at 92.6% x, 93.9% y, 7.4% by 6.1%, fill #01A5A5 at 25% opacity. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients or shadows. Title and section tag in teal #01A5A5 with the section tag label on a lighter teal #78CFCF tab with white text. Body text in dark grey #595959. Box labels in white. Typeface is Bahnschrift, a DIN-style narrow geometric sans-serif, regular weight, uppercase title with normal letter-spacing. Title height about 5.9% of slide height; body and box text about 2.6%. Footer text in light grey #BFBFBF at about 1.5%. Shapes are flat with no rounded corners except the organic TOP MANAGEMENT blob. Thin grey rules about 0.1% of height.

### Slide 43

**Text**

3 / GOVERNANCE / CEO COMPENSATION BREAKDOWN / ANNUAL VARIABLE COMPENSATION / BREAKDOWN OF COMPENSATION / ITEMS UNDER THE 2020 / COMPENSATION POLICY* / LONG-TERM COMPENSATION / Qualitative items / 20% / Safety criteria / Sustainable development / criteria / 50% / 50% / Competitive compensation package / In line with market/industry standards / Compliant with Afep-Medef / recommendations / Waiver by the CEO of his 2020 variable / compensation as part of the Group’s / collective effort in a context / of ongoing health crisis / 26% / 38% / 36% / Fixed compensation / Variable compensation / Long-term compensation / *If performance ceilings are reached / As from 2021, under the long-term incentive / plan, allocation of free shares if the / following performance criteria are met: / up to 50%, targets of adjusted EBITDA / and net financial debt before dividend / payment or share buy-back, calculated / in aggregate over the two preceding / financial years / up to 20%, a target of TSR (Total / Shareholder Return) of Verallia’s share / in relation to the evolution of the share / price of SBF 120 companies on the / regulated market of Euronext Paris / up to 20%, sustainable development / targets, allocated as follows: up to 10%, / a target of decrease in CO2 emissions / and up to 10%, a target of equal pay / up to 10%, a target of ROCE (Return on / Capital Employed) / Quantitative items / 80% / 45% / 55% / Ajusted EBITDA / Operational cash flow / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / verallia / 43

**Layout**

Three-column layout on a white field: a left column of two stacked donut charts with legends (about x 4 to 34), a centre column with a bulleted list, a teal italic-free call-out paragraph and a donut chart with legend (about x 35 to 52), and a right column of an intro paragraph and four bullets (about x 68 to 97). The title sits top-left at x 2.5, y 4.4 above all columns, with a section tag ‘GOVERNANCE’ in the top-left corner. Column headings are at y about 20.5 and sit on a common baseline (the centre heading wraps to three lines). A footnote sits under the centre donut at y 88.2, the footer text is bottom-left at y 96.7, the Verallia logo is bottom-centre, and the page number 43 sits bottom-right on a corner shape. Left margin is about 2.5 to 4 percent and right text edge about 97 percent.

**Visuals**

Three donut charts and one logo. (1) Top-left donut, titled Annual variable compensation, Qualitative items 20%: two half rings. Right half #00A7B9 labelled 50%, left half #52C2CE labelled 50%. Legend beside it: square marker #00A7B9 Safety criteria; square marker #52C2CE Sustainable development criteria. The ring has a white centre hole, a hole about 50 percent of the outer diameter, and no axes or gridlines. (2) Bottom-left donut, Quantitative items 80%: right portion #F36B21 labelled 55% (about 55 percent of the ring), left portion #F9A16F labelled 45%. Legend: square #F36B21 Ajusted EBITDA; square #F9A16F Operational cash flow. White centre hole. (3) Centre donut, Breakdown of compensation items under the 2020 compensation policy, no title beyond the heading: segments clockwise from top: #6C57A6 Fixed compensation 26% (top-right quadrant, labelled in white at its centre), #9B8CC3 Variable compensation 36% (bottom-right), #CFC9E3 Long-term compensation 38% (left side, labelled in white). Segment labels are white text inside each segment. A legend box sits to the right, a pale grey #F2F2F2 rectangle at 39 percent opacity with square markers in the three segment colours in the order Fixed, Variable, Long-term. Footnote beneath: *If performance ceilings are reached. (4) Verallia logo bottom-centre, a stylised green-teal swirl or leaf mark (three overlapping rounded green and teal forms) followed by the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey, 256 by 79 pixels, placed at x 46.1, y 94.6, w 7.8, h 4.3. No photographs or illustrations. Teal accent rules sit under the column headings.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in teal #01A5A5, uppercase, regular weight, Bahnschrift (DIN-style condensed-leaning grotesque sans-serif), about 5.9 percent of slide height. Column headings in grey #7F7F7F, uppercase Bahnschrift regular, about 3.0 percent of slide height. Body text and bullets in grey #7F7F7F Bahnschrift regular about 2.5 percent of slide height; emphasised bullet phrases and call-out text in teal #01A5A5. Bullets are small teal dots in ArialMT. Chart labels in white Bahnschrift about 2.5 percent; legend and small labels in grey about 1.8 percent. Footer text light grey #BFBFBF about 1.5 percent. Section tag GOVERNANCE in white Bahnschrift about 2.0 percent on a pale teal tag #78CFCF; the number 3 in #78CFCF about 3.3 percent. Short teal accent bars (#01A5A5) sit under the column headings. Bottom-right corner has a soft teal quarter-round shape at 25 percent opacity. Shapes have no shadows or gradients; donut rings are flat with no outlines.

### Slide 44

**Text**

3
GOVERNANCE
EXECUTIVE MANAGEMENT COMMITTEE
Verallia’s Executive Committee oversees the
Group’s strategic and operational management
It consists of 10 members, including 2 women
In addition to the CEO, it is composed, at Group
level, of the CFO, the Operations VP, the
Secretary General and the General Counsel and,
at business unit level, of 5 Managing Directors
responsible for the different geographical areas
of the Group, each of them with well-defined
areas of responsibility
Nationalities:
French, Italian, Spanish, Dutch and Mexican
Michel GIANNUZZI
Wendy KOOL-FOULON
Denis MICHEL
Romain BARRAL
Nathalie DELBREUVE
Olivier ROUSSEAU
Marco RAVASI
Quintin TESTA DOMINGUEZ
Paulo PINTO
Hugues DENISSEL
Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021
44

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white slide: a text column on the left (about x 7 to 43 percent) with the body copy, nationality line and flag row, and a cluster of ten circular portraits on a large pale teal blob filling the right side (about x 38 to 100 percent, y 12 to 100 percent). The title sits top-left at x 2.5, y 4.4 under a teal GOVERNANCE tag and section number 3 in the top-left corner. A thin teal rule divides the body text from the nationalities block. Footer text is bottom-left, the verallia logo is bottom-centre, and the page number 44 sits in white at bottom-right on a corner blob. Left margin is about 2.5 to 7 percent.

**Visuals**

Ten black-and-white portrait photographs of executive committee members, each cropped to an irregular rounded blob shape with a teal (#00B3B0) offset blob behind it at about 28 percent opacity, forming a loose ring over a large pale teal blob. Portraits in order: Michel GIANNUZZI (bald smiling man in dark suit, top centre), Wendy KOOL-FOULON (smiling woman with blonde hair, left, in colour-desaturated tone), Denis MICHEL (smiling man with short grey hair in dark suit, upper centre-right), Romain BARRAL (man in grey suit and open collar, centre-left), Nathalie DELBREUVE (smiling woman with shoulder-length dark hair in dark jacket, right), Olivier ROUSSEAU (man in dark suit and open collar, left), Marco RAVASI (man with glasses and tie, centre), Quintin TESTA DOMINGUEZ (man with glasses and dark suit, right), Paulo PINTO (man in dark suit with tie, lower left), Hugues DENISSEL (man with glasses and dark suit, lower right). Lighting is soft and even, frontal, head-and-shoulders framing, no overlays. Five national flag icons in a row beneath the nationalities line, each a round glossy badge: France (blue-white-red vertical), Italy (green-white-red vertical), Spain (red-yellow-red horizontal with coat of arms), Germany (black-red-gold horizontal), Mexico (green-white-red vertical with emblem). Verallia logo at bottom centre: a stylised green-teal leaf-like mark with the word verallia in dark grey lowercase.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with a pale teal blob and teal accents. Title in Bahnschrift-style condensed sans-serif, regular weight, uppercase, teal #01A5A5, about 5.9 percent of slide height. Body text in Bahnschrift regular, grey #7F7F7F, about 3.0 percent of slide height with generous letter spacing and justified alignment. Section tag GOVERNANCE in white on a teal #78CFCF rectangle. Section number 3 in #78CFCF. Name labels in teal #01A5A5 uppercase, about 1.7 percent. Footer in light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.5 percent. Page number white on a corner blob. Shapes are rounded organic blobs with no outlines or shadows; the divider rule is a thin teal line.

Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Three-column grid of equal-width topic blocks across the upper half, each with a teal icon badge above a centred two-line teal heading, a short teal underline and a thin vertical teal rule beneath, with the centred WHISTLEBLOWING heading below and a two-column lower section (left column issues list, right column HOW? list and a teal callout box) separated by a thin vertical rule. The title COMPLIANCE is top-left at about 2.5% from the left edge, the GOVERNANCE tag sits in the top-left corner, the page number is bottom-right on the curved corner shape, the footer text is bottom-left and the Verallia logo is centred at the bottom; left margin about 2.7%, right margin about 4%.

**Visuals**

Three teal circular-blob icon badges (#78CFCF, about 5.5% wide by 9% tall) sit above the column headings: the first shows a white balance scale (competition law), the second shows two white hands holding a small object with radiating lines (prevention of corruption), the third shows a white crossed-out euro-type symbol (embargo and financial sanctions). Three teal underline bars (#01A5A5, about 5% wide, under each heading), three thin vertical teal rules (#78CFCF, about 0.1% wide) under the columns, one thin vertical teal rule divider between the lower columns, and a teal #78CFCF callout box with a white right-pointing chevron outline at its left edge. The Verallia logo (a teal-green leaf-like mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia, image 256 by 79 pixels) sits bottom-centre. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and headings are teal #01A5A5 in a DIN-like geometric sans-serif (Bahnschrift); COMPLIANCE is the largest at about 5.9% of slide height, WHISTLEBLOWING about 4.4%, column headings and lower headings about 2.6 to 3.0%. Body text is grey #7F7F7F Bahnschrift regular at about 2.2% of slide height, with bullet glyphs in teal #01A5A5. Accent colours are teal #01A5A5 and light teal #78CFCF; white text sits on the light teal callout. Shapes are flat with no shadows, no rounded rectangles apart from the blob badges, and the bottom-right corner shape is a quarter-circle in teal at 25% opacity. The footer is light grey #BFBFBF.

**Layout**

Two-column slide on a white field: a rounded photograph occupies the left third with its vertical centre near the middle of the slide, and a thin vertical teal divider separates it from a right text column whose bullets are indented under teal sub-headings. The title sits top-left at about 4.4% from the top, left-aligned at about 2.5% from the left edge, with a small teal section tag above it at the top-left corner. A full-width teal banner sits near the bottom at about 82% to 88% height, with a footer line bottom-left, the Verallia logo bottom-centre and the page number bottom-right in a teal corner shape.

**Visuals**

Photograph (blob-cropped, organic rounded shape): a balance scale still life against a light grey to white seamless background. On the left a small green seedling sprouts from dark brown soil in a shallow grey dish, standing on a thin brown-orange beam. In the centre a gold/ochre triangular fulcrum supports the beam. On the right a stack of gold coins sits on the beam. Soft even studio lighting from above; warm gold and green accents against neutral grey-white. The image is cropped to a rounded blob shape, not full-bleed. A translucent pale teal blob (#01A5A5 at 16% opacity) overlaps the lower-right of the photograph, partly over the beam and fulcrum. Verallia logo: teal-green swirl mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey to its right, 256 by 79 pixel image.

**Design**

Background plain white #FFFFFF. Title in Bahnschrift regular, all caps, teal #01A5A5, about 5.9% of slide height. Section tag text in Bahnschrift regular, white #FFFFFF, all caps, on a light teal #78CFCF box; section number 3 in #78CFCF. Lead lines in Bahnschrift regular teal #01A5A5 at about 3.0% of slide height; body and bullet text in Bahnschrift regular grey #7F7F7F at about 2.6%; bullet glyphs are teal #01A5A5 Arial-style round dots. Banner text is Bahnschrift regular, all caps, white on #78CFCF. Footer text Bahnschrift light grey #BFBFBF at about 1.5%. Page number Bahnschrift white at about 2.2%. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; the photo and translucent blob have organic rounded outlines; banner is a rectangle with a left-pointing notch in a thin teal outline.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a funnel diagram occupies the left column beneath a centred section heading, and a 2x2 grid of numbered steps fills the right column beneath its own centred heading. The title sits top-left at about 4.4% from the top with the subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned at a margin of about 2.5% to 3% of slide width. A section tag GOVERNANCE sits in the top-left corner, the footer line and teal bar sit bottom-left, the Verallia logo sits bottom-centre, and the page number 47 sits bottom-right on a corner shape.

**Visuals**

Funnel diagram on the left: four stacked trapezoidal segments narrowing downward, each shaped as a flat-topped inverted trapezoid with small downward chevron notches between segments. Segment 1 (top) is a pale teal band with black text Supplier Risk Mapping / AFNOR CSR risk mapping tool. Segment 2 is a mid-light teal band with black text Assessment / Questionnaire / ECOVADIS or ACESIA. Segment 3 is a deeper teal band with black text On site CSR / audit / QIMA. Below the funnel tip is a solid teal rectangle labelled Progress plan / Blacklist in white. Four white rounded speech-bubble badges with teal numerals 1, 2, 3 and 4 sit at the left edge of segments 1, 2 and 3 and the rectangle. Right column: four numbered steps, each with a large teal numeral and a thin vertical teal rule beside its text block. Step 1 text is grey, with the highlighted teal phrases 260 suppliers identified as and “potential risk supplier”. Step 2 text is grey, with teal 240 suppliers already assessed or under evaluation process. Step 3 text is grey, with a teal highlighted block Audit campaign started in Q4 2020 aiming at inspecting more than 50 suppliers by end of 2021. Step 4 text is grey: Corrective action plan management. Bottom-centre: Verallia logo, a teal circular swirl mark overlapping a green leaf-like shape, followed by the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Title in teal #01A5A5, all caps, regular weight Bahnschrift (DIN-style condensed sans-serif), about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle and step body text in grey #7F7F7F regular Bahnschrift, about 2.6% to 3.3% of slide height. Teal accents #01A5A5 for section headings, numerals, rules and highlight phrases. Funnel bands use teal #01A5A5 at opacities 0.20, 0.50 and 0.80 plus solid teal for the bottom bar. Section tag uses light teal #78CFCF fill with white text. Footer text is light grey #BFBFBF. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients and square corners except the rounded badges.
