### Slide 48

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear route: an agenda that is repeated as a tracker (slides 2, 3, 10, 39, 48), consistent section tags, and three pillars each opened by a photo divider and followed by goal tables with 2019 baselines and 2025 targets.

**Text**

GENDA / INTRODUCTION / CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS / GOVERNANCE / CONCLUSION / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Laetitia FABRE / Head of CSR / Romain BARRAL / Director of Operations / Denis MICHEL / Secretary General / Wendy KOOL-FOULON / General Counsel / Michel GIANNUZZI / CEO / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 48

**Layout**

Agenda slide with a four-column grid of equal-width sections spanning the slide, each with a blob-framed image at top, a short horizontal rule, a section heading, and a portrait with name and role below. The title GENDA sits top-left at about x 9.9 and y 5.8, left-aligned, with a white letter A preceding it at x 6.3 (invisible on white). The Verallia logo is centred at the bottom around x 46 to 54, y 94 to 99. The footer text sits bottom-left at x 2.8, y 96.7. The slide number 48 sits bottom-right on a corner shape at x 95.9, y 96.8. Margins are about 3 percent on left and right.

**Visuals**

Four blob-framed image groups in a row. Column 1 (INTRODUCTION): a greyscale photograph of glass bottles and jars of varied shapes, standing and lying, in a light-grey tone, inside a grey organic blob ring; full-bleed within a rounded blob mask; faded to about 30 percent contrast. Column 2 (CSR STRATEGY: 3 MAIN PILLARS): a split greyscale photograph, left half a clear glass bottle on a dark background, right half a close-up of leaves or foliage, with a thin white curved line tracing a figure-eight across the centre; masked in a blob; faded. Column 3 (GOVERNANCE): a grey line icon of a circular loop with four arms ending in cog-like knobs, arranged around a central ring with gaps, on a white background inside a grey blob ring. Column 4 (CONCLUSION): a full-colour photograph of green, blue and clear glass bottles of varied heights standing on a wooden surface with a clear jar at lower left, against a pale grey background; cropped by a teal blob mask; at full strength while other columns are faded. Portraits: five greyscale head-and-shoulders photographs of smiling people in dark jackets, each in a faded oval blob mask; the Conclusion portrait of Michel Giannuzzi is in full greyscale with a teal #01A5A5 blob behind it. One small Verallia logo in colour (teal and green overlapping circles with dark grey wordmark) centred at the bottom. Photo for Wendy KOOL-FOULON is a light-haired woman in a light top.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title GENDA in teal #01A5A5, all capitals, Bahnschrift regular, about 10 percent of slide height. Inactive section headings in light grey #7F7F7F, all capitals, Bahnschrift regular, about 3 percent of slide height; active CONCLUSION heading in #7F7F7F with the active rule in teal #01A5A5. Inactive names and roles in #7F7F7F and #BFBFBF, Bahnschrift regular, about 1.5 to 1.7 percent of slide height; active name and role in teal #01A5A5. Footer text in #BFBFBF Bahnschrift regular about 1.5 percent. Page number 48 in white Bahnschrift about 2.2 percent. Horizontal rules are 2.5 percent-wide thin bars: inactive #BFBFBF, active #01A5A5. Blob rings are soft grey #E9EAED and #E4E6E9 organic shapes with transparency and an active teal #78CFCF blob. Bottom-left teal bar #78CFCF under footer. Bottom-right corner shape in pale teal #01A5A5 at 25 percent opacity with a rounded top-left curve.

**Layout**

Single-column header band with a section tag and title at top left, a full-width photographic band across the upper middle (about 14% to 48% of the height), two text columns below separated by a thin vertical divider at about 43.5% of the width (left column centred on about 22.5% of the width, right column centred on about 71%), a full-width teal banner with a left chevron at about 85% to 91% of the height, and a footer row with the source line at bottom left, the Verallia logo centred at bottom and the page number at bottom right on a light teal corner shape. Left margin for the title is about 2.5% of the width; the slide has no visible slide-number box other than the bottom-right corner shape.

**Visuals**

Photograph: full-width band across the slide, cropped to the band edges (no border), showing a close-up of crushed glass cullet in greens, whites and greys in the left and centre, with blurred green glass bottles and ring-shaped bottle rims at the right; soft natural light, dominant colours green, white and olive-brown; a black overlay at 44% opacity darkens the band behind the quotation text. Quotation mark: a large white opening double quotation mark (a 328 by 297 px image) sits on the photo at the left of the quote text, roughly 12% to 27% of the width. Five further placed images (608 by 149 px, 327 by 152 px, 172 by 152 px, 124 by 152 px, 328 by 297 px as listed in measurements) lie within the quote area with no distinguishable visible content; they appear to be transparent effect layers behind the quote text. Vertical divider: a thin teal line between the two columns, about 0.1% of the width, running from about 51% to 81% of the height. Chevron: a thin teal outline arrow tip with a vertical line at the left edge of the banner, about 81% to 94% of the height. Logo: Verallia logo at bottom centre, a green-teal overlapping-drop mark with the lowercase wordmark 'verallia' in dark grey. Corner shape: light teal quarter-circle at bottom right behind the page number. Bottom-left bar: short solid teal bar under the footer text.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF solid background with a full-width dimmed photograph of glass cullet in the band from about 14% to 48% of the height. White background with a dark-tinted photographic band. Title 'OUR ESG AMBITION' in teal #01A5A5, all capitals, Bahnschrift regular (DIN-style grotesque sans-serif), size about 5.9% of slide height. Section tag text 'CONCLUSION' in white capitals on a light teal rectangle #78CFCF, with the number '4' in light teal #78CFCF to its left. Quotation text 'Re-imagine for a sustainable future' in white Bahnschrift regular, about 8.9% of slide height, with the word 'glass' larger at about 14.8% of slide height. Body and column text in teal #01A5A5 Bahnschrift regular at about 3.3% of slide height, centred. Column captions in grey #7F7F7F Bahnschrift regular at about 2.0% of slide height, centred. Banner text in white Bahnschrift regular capitals at about 3.7% of slide height. Footer text in light grey #BFBFBF Bahnschrift regular at about 1.5% of slide height. Page number '49' in white at about 2.2% of slide height. Accents: teal #01A5A5 and light teal #78CFCF. Banner: flat solid #78CFCF rectangle with square corners, no shadow, no gradient. Divider and chevron are hairline teal strokes. No shadows or gradients on text.

### Slide 50

**Text**

4 / CONCLUSION / LATEST RATINGS AND PARTNERSHIPS / CDP / DISCLOSURE INSIGHT ACTION / A- / Since 2020 / Top 5% / Score: 67/100 / GOLD / 2020 / ecovadis / Sustainability / Rating / Since 2013 / DRIVING AMBITIOUS CORPORATE CLIMATE ACTION / SCIENCE / BASED / TARGETS / “COMMITTED” / status / Since 2020 / verallia / Since 2020 / SUSTAINALYTICS / Low risk / Since 2016 / Sedex / ® / UN GLOBAL COMPACT / Since 2016 / ELLEN MACARTHUR / FOUNDATION / Member / Since 2020 / Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021 / 50

**Layout**

Single-slide hub-and-spoke layout on a white field: the title LATEST RATINGS AND PARTNERSHIPS is left-aligned at the top-left under a small teal section tab, with the centre Verallia logo blob placed at the middle of the slide and six partner logos arranged around it (CDP top centre, EcoVadis top left, UN Global Compact lower left, Ellen MacArthur Foundation lower centre-left, Sedex lower right, Sustainalytics right, Science Based Targets upper right), each joined to the hub by a thin teal elbow connector ending in a small dot and labelled with a ‘Since’ year. Footer text sits bottom-left at about 2.8% margin, the page number 50 sits bottom-right on a pale teal corner shape; there is no separate footer rule beyond a short teal bar at bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Hub-and-spoke diagram with six logos and one central logo. Centre: a grey #E9EAED blob (about 18% wide, 32% tall) behind a teal #78CFCF ring-shaped blob with a white inner blob, carrying the Verallia logo (teal and green overlapping droplet mark with dark grey wordmark ‘verallia’). Connectors: thin teal #01A5A5 lines of about 1 px, each with a small filled teal dot at the logo end and elbow bends; labels in italic teal ‘Since’ text. Spokes: (1) CDP logo, red and dark red (#B01E2D area) geometric mark with ‘CDP’ wordmark and ‘DISCLOSURE INSIGHT ACTION’ in red caps, with large dark red ‘A-’ beneath; line from CDP dot runs right then down to the hub; label Since 2020. (2) EcoVadis gold medal badge, about 12% wide, round, gold ring with gold ribbon banner reading GOLD, white centre with ‘2020 ecovadis Sustainability Rating’, text ‘Top 5%’ and ‘Score: 67/100’ in grey above; horizontal line to hub labelled Since 2013. (3) Science Based Targets logo: circular emblem with thin multicoloured arcs (teal, blue, orange, red) and text SCIENCE BASED TARGETS in dark grey sans-serif, with caption DRIVING AMBITIOUS CORPORATE CLIMATE ACTION and ‘“COMMITTED” status’ in grey; line from hub to its dot labelled Since 2020. (4) Sustainalytics logo: concentric quarter-circle icon of yellow-orange bars and rings (#F5B041 to #FFC000 range, pale orange at right) with bold dark grey SUSTAINALYTICS wordmark and grey ‘Low risk’; horizontal line labelled Since 2020. (5) Sedex logo: bold dark grey ‘Sedex’ wordmark with a crimson-red circle (#D7294B) overlapping the x and a small ® mark; line labelled Since 2016. (6) Ellen MacArthur Foundation Member logo: black concentric rings mark with bold black caps ELLEN MACARTHUR FOUNDATION and serif ‘Member’; line labelled Since 2020. (7) UN Global Compact emblem: dark navy blue globe with white meridians inside a laurel wreath, with UN GLOBAL COMPACT arched in dark navy caps; line labelled Since 2016. Lighting flat, no photographs; all logos at full colour on white, unboxed.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Title in teal #01A5A5 Bahnschrift regular caps with generous letter-spacing, about 5.9% of slide height. Section tab: teal #78CFCF box with white caps ‘CONCLUSION’ in Bahnschrift about 2% of slide height and a teal numeral 4 at the far left. Labels in italic teal #78CFCE Bahnschrift about 2.2% of slide height; grey #7F7F7F Bahnschrift for score and caption text; red #C00000 bold Bahnschrift for ‘A-’ at about 10% of slide height. Footer text #BFBFBF Bahnschrift about 1.5%. Connectors thin teal, no shadows or gradients; the bottom-right corner shape is pale teal #01A5A5 at 25% opacity with a curved top-left edge.

Verallia’s ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a white slide: a teal-ringed, rounded-triangle photo cluster sits in the left half, and the large Q&A title sits vertically centred in the right half, left-aligned. The footer runs along the bottom-left margin with the Verallia logo centred at the bottom and the page number in a light teal corner shape at the bottom-right. No header and no visible slide grid lines.

**Visuals**

Photo inside a rounded-triangle teal frame: left half shows an amber-orange glass bottle with a glowing warm background of a glass factory and industrial dark machinery, with a thin white infinity-style loop line crossing the centre of the image; right half shows brown tree bark with green ivy leaves. The photo is cropped to a rounded-triangular blob and sits on a #78CFCF teal ring, which sits on a light grey #E9EAED blob behind it. Lighting is warm and directional on the bottle; dominant colours are amber orange, dark brown, bark grey-brown and leaf green. Verallia logo at bottom centre: a teal-green swoosh mark with the word verallia in dark grey lowercase. No other graphics.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with organic blob shapes in teal and light grey. Title Q&A in Bahnschrift regular, teal #00A7A7, cap height roughly 25 percent of slide height. Footer text in Bahnschrift regular, light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.5 percent of slide height. Page number in Bahnschrift regular white #FFFFFF on a light teal corner shape. Teal accent bar #78CFCF at bottom left under the footer. Blob shapes have smooth, soft curves with no shadows; the photo sits in a clean teal border with no visible shadow line.

### Slide 53

**Text**

APPENDICES
WATER, WASTE AND EMISSIONS MANAGEMENT
WATER
• Glass packaging plants are not big water consumers,
as water is mainly used as a coolant of equipment or
hot glass rejections
• However Verallia has developed technical solutions
to limit water consumption of its plants through
efficient closed circuits, elimination of evaporation
and monitoring of losses
>> Solutions in place in 44% of the group plants,
>> allowing water consumption below 0.4m³/ton
of packed glass
• Verallia is upgrading the remaining factories, putting
in priority the ones located in areas in high hydric
stress
WASTES
• Glass packaging plants are not generating significant
volumes of waste, as rejects from production are
mainly made of glass which is recycled
• Verallia focus is on recycling those wastes, including
the furnace elements after reconstruction. In 2020,
60% of Verallia wastes were recycled
EMISSIONS
• Gas emissions (NOx, SOx) resulting from furnace
operations are subject to strict regulations, and Verallia
implemented close monitoring in each of its plants to
ensure compliance
• In addition, combustion optimization activities conducted
by R&D and Continuous Improvement teams are aiming
at reducing further those emissions
3
53
Verallia's ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021

**Layout**

Two text columns on a white slide: a left column for WATER starting at about x 5% and a right column starting at about x 51.6% with WASTES above EMISSIONS, both starting at the same height (about y 23%). The title WATER, WASTE AND EMISSIONS MANAGEMENT sits left-aligned at the top (about y 4.4%) beneath a small APPENDICES tag in the top-left corner; the title is not centred and there is a large empty gap between title and body. The footer line sits bottom-left, the Verallia logo is bottom-centre, and the page number 53 sits bottom-right on a corner shape, with margins of about 2.5% on the left.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: (1) Verallia logo at bottom centre (about x 46.1%, y 94.6%, 7.8% by 4.3%), showing a green-teal stylised overlapping drop or leaf mark with the lowercase wordmark verallia in dark grey to its right; (2) a light teal quarter-circle shape in the bottom-right corner (fill #01A5A5 at 25% opacity, rendered as pale teal) behind the page number; (3) a small solid teal bar at the bottom-left under the footer text (about x 2.5%, y 98.9%, 9.7% by 1.1%, estimated colour #01A5A5); (4) a light teal tag rectangle behind the APPENDICES label at the top-left (about x 2.8%, y 0%, 11% by 3.6%, estimated colour #6CC5C5). No photographs, charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). Title and section headings WATER, WASTES, EMISSIONS in teal #01A5A5, uppercase Bahnschrift regular (a DIN-style condensed-humanist sans-serif). Body bullet text is mid grey #7F7F7F in Bahnschrift regular, with bullets as teal #01A5A5 '•' glyphs in Arial and '>>' sub-markers in teal #00A7A7. Footer text light grey #BFBFBF in Bahnschrift. Title is about 5.9% of slide height, section headings about 3.3%, body about 3.0%, footer about 1.5%. Line spacing is about 1.15. Bullets are hanging with a small indent; sub-bullets are indented further. Thin lines and shapes: none apart from the flat teal tag and corner shapes; no gradients, shadows or rounded boxes.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#FFFFFF]: Full-slide white background fill (Covers the whole slide, behind everything)

– January 21, 2021 / 54

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a small teal APPENDICES tag at the top left, the title VERALLIA ESG GOALS directly below it at the left margin (about 2.5% from left, about 4.4% from top), and a full-width table occupying roughly 2.7% to 97.5% of the width and 12.9% to 92.5% of the height. The table has seven columns (Our Purpose, Our Commitments, Alignment with the UN SDGs, Our Goals, Performance indicators, 2019, 2025 Targets) with a teal header row, a teal rule closes the table at the bottom, the footer text sits bottom left, the Verallia logo bottom centre, and the page number bottom right on a light teal corner accent.

**Visuals**

Table: seven columns, header row in teal #01A5A5 with white centred labels. Column 1 'Our Purpose' is a tall teal #78CFCE cell (x2.7 to 13.1, y18.7 to 92.6) holding the text 'Re-imagine glass for a sustainable future' in white. Column 2 'Our Commitments' holds three merged group labels in teal text: 'Enhance the circularity of glass packaging' (rows 1 to 3), 'Significantly reduce our CO2 emissions across our operations' (rows 4 to 6), 'Provide a safe and inclusive place to work' (rows 7 to 10). Column 3 'Alignment with the UN SDGs' holds nine SDG icons in three groups. Column 4 'Our Goals' and column 5 'Performance indicators' hold bulleted text in grey with teal bullets. Column 6 '2019' and column 7 '2025 Targets' hold centred values. Rows 1 to 3 and rows 7 to 10 are white; rows 4 to 6 are pale teal #C9EAEB; thin white horizontal dividers separate rows; the table ends with a teal #01A5A5 rule at about 92.5% height. Row-by-row values: row 1 Enable the increase cullet collection by 7 pts | % of domestic used glass collected in Verallia's countries in Europe | 2019 76% (2018 figure) | 2025 83%. Row 2 Increase the rate of use of external cullet by 10 pts | Rate of external cullet usage in our glass production sites | 49% | 59%. Row 3 Expand viable Reuse business models | Test a pilot to validate the business model | N/A | At least 1 pilot in France. Row 4 Reduce our absolute CO2 emissions (scopes 1 & 2) by 27.5% by 2030 vs 2019, in line with Science Based Targets | Tons of CO2 emitted (scopes 1 & 2) | 3,090 k | 2,626 k. Row 5 Revert the trend by reducing average weight of our standard and non returnable bottles and jars by 3% | Alpha index (= weight / volume^0.8 as per NF-H35077 norm) | 16 | 15.5. Row 6 Plant 100,000 trees per year; and offset all professional travels emissions every year | 100,000 trees planted; Number of certified carbon credits | 100,000; 30,910 | 700,000 TBD (> total CO2 emissions linked to professional travels). Row 7 Aim for 'zero accident' every year | TF2 (= all accidents / million hours worked) | 5.5 | < 2. Row 8 Increase gender equality in all Verallia countries by 15 pts | Gender equality index (as defined by French law) | 60 | 75. Row 9 Favour insertion of disabled people by doubling the ratio of disabled employees | % of disabled people (according to national definitions) | 3% | 6%. Row 10 Encourage employees shareholding ownership | % of Verallia share capital held by employees (directly or through FCPE) | 2.6% | 5%. SDG icons, all square-cornered tiles about 3.6% wide by 6.3% high, dark-blue #1F3F66 tiles for SDG 17 and bright tiles for the others: SDG 13 Climate Action (green #3E7D3A, white globe-and-eye pictogram) in rows 1, 4; SDG 12 Responsible Consumption and Production (gold #C58F2F, white infinity pictogram) in row 2; SDG 17 Partnerships for the Goals (navy #1F3F66, white interlinked-circles pictogram) in rows 3, 6, 9; SDG 15 Life on Land (green #5BA83A, white tree and sun pictogram) in row 5; SDG 8 Decent Work and Economic Growth (maroon #8C1E3C, white rising bar-chart arrow) in row 7; SDG 5 Gender Equality (red #E0412E, white female-male symbol) in row 8. Logo: Verallia wordmark in grey with a stylised green-teal leaf/drop mark, not cropped, boxed by nothing.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

Market-based method = based on GHG emissions emitted by the generators from which Verallia contractually purchases electricity bundled with contractual instruments, or contractual instruments on their own. "Location-based" method was used until 2019 = based on average energy generation emission factors for defined geographic locations, including local, subnational, or national boundaries. 2019 Scope 2 "location-based" = 487,825 tons, "market-based" = 610,653 tons
Verallia's ESG Strategy Presentation – January 21, 2021
55

**Layout**

A full-width table of 7 columns (Our Purpose, Our Commitments, Our Goals, Performance indicators, 2018, 2019, 2025 Targets) starts below a title at top-left, with an APPENDICES tag above the title at the top-left margin. The table runs from about x 2.7% to 97.5% and y 11.3% to 88.9%, with a footnote band beneath it, a footer line bottom-left, the Verallia logo bottom-centre and the page number 55 bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Table with 7 columns and header row: Our Purpose | Our Commitments | Our Goals | Performance indicators | 2018 | 2019 | 2025 Targets. Left column 'Our Purpose' is one merged teal cell spanning all body rows with the white text 'Re-imagine glass for a sustainable future'. Commitments column groups: 'Enhance the circularity of glass packaging' (rows 1-3), 'Significantly reduce our CO2 emissions across our operations' (rows 4-6), 'Provide a safe and inclusive place to work' (rows 7-10). Rows: (1) Enable the increase cullet collection by 7 pts | % of domestic used glass collected in Verallia's countries in Europe | 2018 76% | 2019 76% (2018 figure) | 2025 83%. (2) Increase the rate of use of external cullet by 10 pts | Rate of external cullet usage in our glass production sites | 47% | 49% | 59%. (3) Expand viable Reuse business models | Test a pilot to validate the business model | N/A | N/A | At least 1 pilot in France. (4) Reduce our absolute CO2 emissions (scopes 1 & 2) by 27.5% by 2030 vs 2019, in line with Science Based Targets | Tons of CO2 emitted (scopes 1 & 2) | 3,045 k (scope 2 location-based)(1) | 3,090 k (scope 2 market-based)(1) = 2.967 k (location-based) | 2,626 k (scope 2 market-based). (5) Revert the trend by reducing average weight of our standard and non returnable bottles and jars by 3% | Alpha index (= weight / volume^0.8 as per NF-H35077 norm) | 15.9 | 16 | 15.5. (6) Plant 100,000 trees per year and offset all professional travels emissions every year | 100,000 trees planted (0 | 100,000 | 700,000) and Number of certified carbon credits (0 | 30,910 | TBD > total CO2 emissions linked to professional travels Y-1). (7) Aim for 'zero accident' every year | TF2 (= all accidents / million hours worked) | 4.8 | 5.5 | < 2. (8) Increase gender equality in all Verallia countries by 15 pts | Gender equality index (as defined by French law) | N/A | 60 | 75. (9) Favour insertion of disabled people by doubling the ratio of disabled employees | % of disabled people (according to national definitions) | blank | 3% | 6%. (10) Encourage employees shareholding ownership | % of Verallia share capital held by employees (directly or through FCPE) | blank | 2.6% | 5%. Footnote (1) text in small grey type below the table. No charts, photographs or logos apart from the Verallia logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look on a white background. Teal #01A5A5 is used for the APPENDICES tag, the title, the table header row and the commitment labels; pale teal #78CFCE fills the Purpose column and #C9EAEB fills alternating commitment bands; white rows separate the other bands. Body text is dark grey #595959, footnote grey #7F7F7F, footer #BFBFBF. Typeface is Bahnschrift (a DIN-style condensed-leaning sans-serif) in regular weight throughout, with the title in all caps at about 5.9% of slide height and body text about 2.0%. Calibri appears on a few lines of the share capital row and ArialMT for bullets. Cells are separated by thin teal vertical rules; no rounded corners or shadows. A pale teal curved shape sits bottom-right at 0.25 opacity.
